**[00:02:20.980] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000018)  
Honorable members, team colleagues, good afternoon.

**[00:02:27.080] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000019)  
I would like to welcome everyone to this budget hearing of the Civil Service Commission,

**[00:02:34.620] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000020)  
including the Career Executive Service Board, headed by Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap.

**[00:02:47.640] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000021)  
As the agency responsible for managing and strengthening the government's human resources,

**[00:02:56.060] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000022)  
the CSC recognizes the need to continuously modernize its systems

**[00:03:03.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000023)  
and processes. Digital transformation

**[00:03:07.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000024)  
Digital transformation is no longer simply an option.

**[00:03:12.600] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000025)  
It is essential to providing faster, more efficient, and more accessible government services.

**[00:03:23.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000026)  
For fiscal year 2027, the CSC is proposing a total budget of 3.89 billion.

**[00:03:33.980] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000027)  
This represents

**[00:03:37.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000028)  
an increase of 1.14 billion, or 41.4% from the fiscal year 2026

**[00:03:48.780] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000029)  
approved budget of 2.89 billion.

**[00:03:54.200] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000030)  
A significant portion of this proposed increase, amounting to $1.453 billion,

**[00:04:03.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000031)  
is extended for the implementation...

**[00:04:07.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000032)  
of the Philippine Civil Service Modernization Project, PCSMP,

**[00:04:15.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000033)  
a parent-assisted project that will support the modernization of our human resource system.

**[00:04:25.540] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000034)  
Among its key initiatives are the rollout of Prime Embedded Systems Governance, ESG,

**[00:04:35.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000035)  
the HRMIS payroll system, and the development of a national learning management system.

**[00:04:46.600] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000036)  
These initiatives are intended to make human resource services more efficient,

**[00:04:55.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000037)  
accessible, and responsive,

**[00:04:58.260] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000038)  
while also improving the delivery of services to government employees,

**[00:05:05.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000039)  
and the public.

**[00:05:06.960] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000040)  
I will enable the CAC to maintain reliable, secure, and continuous operation

**[00:05:15.700] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000041)  
while advancing the modernization of the civil service.

**[00:05:20.780] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000042)  
With the continued trust and support of Congress,

**[00:05:24.980] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000043)  
the Civil Service Commission will be better positioned

**[00:05:27.940] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000044)  
to lead the modernization of human resource governance

**[00:05:33.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000045)  
in the Philippine bureaucracy,

**[00:05:35.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000046)  
and contribute to a more efficient, responsive,

**[00:05:40.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000047)  
and citizen-centered government.

**[00:05:43.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000048)  
Thank you very much, and we look forward to a productive and meaningful discussion.

**[00:05:52.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000049)  
Chairperson Marilyn Yap, you may now proceed with your presentation.

**[00:06:07.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000050)  
Hello.

**[00:06:11.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000051)  
Deputy Majority Leader and Appropriations Committee Vice Chairperson,

**[00:06:24.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000060)  
Representative Dada Kiram Pismula, Representative Emery Lollipop Uano Dizon, Representative Angela Garcia, Representative Dante Garcia, Representative Choboy Aquino, and all our honorable representatives who are attending this meeting online.

**[00:06:48.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000061)  
Magandang hapon po sa ating lahat.

**[00:06:50.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000062)  
We are pleased to present this afternoon the proposed 2027 budget of the Civil Service Commission along with highlights of our accomplishments for the year 2024 to the first semester 2025 and up to the first semester of 2026 and including our targets for 2027.

**[00:07:15.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000063)  
Ang amin pong proposed budget ay maglalahad ng mga programa upang panaginipan.

**[00:07:20.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000064)  
Magtuloy naming mapagbuti ang pagtupad ng aming mandato.

**[00:07:24.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000065)  
Nasa slide po ngayon ang mandato ng Civil Service Commission to be the central personal agency of the government tasked to establish a career service and adopt measures to promote moral efficiency, integrity, responsiveness, progressiveness, and courtesy in the civil service.

**[00:07:45.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000066)  
It is also tasked, our commission is also tasked to strengthen the merit.

**[00:07:50.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000067)  
and reward system, integrate all human resource development programs for all levels and ranks, and institutionalize a management climate in government conducive to public accountability.

**[00:08:08.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000068)  
Sa kanyang 2026 State of the Nation address, nanawagan ang Pangulong Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr. na ipagpatuloy natin ang nasimulan nating mga gawain upang maibigay sa ating mga kababayan,

**[00:08:21.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000069)  
ang pansang karapatdapat sa kanila.

**[00:08:24.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000070)  
Ito ay nagsisilbing inspirasyon para sa amin para patuloy naming pagsikapan at pagbutihin ang aming mga programa upang ang pamahalaan ay patuloy na maging karapatdapat

**[00:08:36.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000071)  
sa pagtitiwala ng pamamayan.

**[00:08:42.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000072)  
Sa ngayon po, as of June 2026 the Philippine Civil Service Workforce is more than 2.25 million strong,

**[00:08:50.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000073)  
consisting of 2.02 million career professionals and 232,770-plus non-career professionals.

**[00:09:00.380] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000074)  
Ito po ang distribution across sectors ng ating career and non-career professionals,

**[00:09:05.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000075)  
sectors meaning the national government agencies, the state universities and colleges,

**[00:09:10.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000076)  
local government units, government-owned and controlled corporations,

**[00:09:14.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000077)  
kasama na po ang mga local water districts.

**[00:09:17.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000078)  
Pag-tignan niyo po ang mga datos natin,

**[00:09:20.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000079)  
73.6% of career professionals work in national government agencies,

**[00:09:27.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000080)  
while 18.5% work in local government units.

**[00:09:31.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000081)  
On the other hand, 60.6% of non-career professionals work in LGUs or local government units,

**[00:09:39.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000082)  
while only 29.7% work in national government agencies.

**[00:09:44.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000083)  
In addition,

**[00:09:47.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000084)  
there are also workers in the government workforce which are called job order and contract of service workers.

**[00:10:00.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000085)  
Today, there are more than 1 million job order and contract of service workers in government.

**[00:10:05.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000086)  
The majority of JOs and COs are in local government units,

**[00:10:12.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000087)  
while only 22.14% are in national government.

**[00:10:17.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000088)  
So, all together, the government workforce is more than 3.29 million,

**[00:10:24.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000089)  
composed of 2.021 million professionals,

**[00:10:29.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000090)  
232,770 plus non-career professionals,

**[00:10:34.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000091)  
and 1,038,204 job order and contract of service workers.

**[00:10:43.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000092)  
Ang ating civil service workforce,

**[00:10:47.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000093)  
ay ito po ang aming workforce sa civil service.

**[00:10:51.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000094)  
The CSC has only a total personnel allocation of 1,690 positions.

**[00:10:57.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000095)  
Of this number, 1,541 are filled,

**[00:11:01.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000096)  
consisting of 1,260 career employees,

**[00:11:04.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000097)  
49 career employees,

**[00:11:06.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000098)  
and 241 JOs and contracts of service workers.

**[00:11:12.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000099)  
149 positions in our agency remain unfilled or vacant.

**[00:11:17.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000100)  
Despite our nationwide mandate,

**[00:11:20.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000101)  
there is only one civil service second-level and up employee

**[00:11:24.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000102)  
for every 3,199 government workers.

**[00:11:29.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000103)  
In comparison, COA has 13,310 personnel,

**[00:11:35.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000104)  
COMELEC has 6,971,

**[00:11:37.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000105)  
and the CSC has only a total personnel allocation of 1,690.

**[00:11:43.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000106)  
Our proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[00:11:48.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000107)  
embodies our commitment to strengthen the civil service

**[00:11:52.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000108)  
through our three program pillars,

**[00:11:55.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000109)  
namely human resource governance,

**[00:11:58.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000110)  
professionalization and workplace cooperation,

**[00:12:01.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000111)  
and administrative justice.

**[00:12:03.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000112)  
For human resource governance,

**[00:12:07.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000113)  
ang una pong sub-program,

**[00:12:12.350] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000114)  
ang human resource governance,

**[00:12:14.910] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000115)  
ay ito po ang mga policy reforms

**[00:12:16.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000116)  
that have been implemented for the period mentioned earlier.

**[00:12:20.710] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000117)  
The CSC continues to implement the 2025 Omnibus Rules and Appointments

**[00:12:26.170] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000118)  
and other human resource actions, or ORA-ORA,

**[00:12:29.550] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000119)  
which now allows for digital signatures to be affixed

**[00:12:32.950] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000120)  
on Civil Service Commission records.

**[00:12:35.950] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000121)  
Online oath-taking and online submission of appointments

**[00:12:39.830] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000122)  
are also already allowed.

**[00:12:41.830] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000123)  
This is part of the shift that is integral

**[00:12:46.590] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000124)  
to our moves for digital transformation in the Commission.

**[00:12:49.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000125)  
We also issued the 2025 Rules and Administrative Cases

**[00:12:53.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000126)  
in the Civil Service

**[00:12:54.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000127)  
and the 2025 Omnibus Rules and SALN.

**[00:12:57.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000128)  
And today, we now allow digital signatures

**[00:13:02.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000129)  
in terms of cases, pati-oath-taking, digital na rin po.

**[00:13:06.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000130)  
And then, the Omnibus Rules and SALN

**[00:13:09.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000131)  
provides for digital SALN forms, submission online

**[00:13:13.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000132)  
of statement of assets, liabilities, and net worth.

**[00:13:19.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000133)  
We also expanded the project

**[00:13:21.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000134)  
with preferential ratings for qualified job orders,

**[00:13:25.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000135)  
contract of service, and other covered government workers.

**[00:13:29.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000136)  
Preferential ratings were provided for,

**[00:13:32.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000137)  
not only for JOs and COs,

**[00:13:34.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000138)  
but also for military and uniformed personnel

**[00:13:37.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000139)  
and overseas Filipino workers.

**[00:13:40.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000140)  
In addition, the Commission has approved the reduction

**[00:13:43.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000141)  
of the experience requirement for JOs and COs

**[00:13:46.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000142)  
from the 10 years in previous years

**[00:13:49.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000143)  
to five years.

**[00:13:51.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000144)  
The Commission has also approved the reduction

**[00:13:53.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000145)  
of the experience in government service.

**[00:13:55.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000146)  
We also have a policy to enable K-12 graduates

**[00:14:01.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000147)  
to qualify for first-level positions in government.

**[00:14:05.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000148)  
We also granted, pursuant to the law,

**[00:14:08.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000149)  
an SK autonomy,

**[00:14:10.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000150)  
the grant of eligibility to Sangguniang Kabataan officials.

**[00:14:14.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000151)  
The latest is the Wellness Leave Policy

**[00:14:18.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000152)  
and the Gambling Prohibition Policy.

**[00:14:20.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000153)  
The Wellness Leave Policy gives five days

**[00:14:23.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000154)  
wellness leave to all government employees,

**[00:14:26.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000155)  
and the Gambling Prohibition Policy inhibits

**[00:14:29.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000156)  
or prescribes any form of gambling

**[00:14:32.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000157)  
among government employees,

**[00:14:34.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000158)  
whether this be on-site or online.

**[00:14:37.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000159)  
We are also pursuing major reforms

**[00:14:39.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000160)  
to modernize the civil service.

**[00:14:41.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000161)  
These include the review and overhaul

**[00:14:44.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000162)  
of our qualification standards,

**[00:14:46.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000163)  
which are more than 30 years old

**[00:14:48.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000164)  
and are no longer applicable

**[00:14:50.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000165)  
to the present requirements of the government service.

**[00:14:53.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000166)  
This program is being undertaken

**[00:14:55.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000167)  
with the University of the Philippines

**[00:14:57.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000168)  
National College of Public Administration

**[00:15:00.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000169)  
And the focus is in shifting qualification standards to competency-based standards.

**[00:15:07.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000170)  
We are also reviewing our civil service examinations with the possible shift also of the civil

**[00:15:18.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000171)  
service examinations from aptitude-based exams to competency-based examinations.

**[00:15:25.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000172)  
We are also laying the groundwork for the establishment of the National Civil Service

**[00:15:29.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000173)  
Leadership and Management Development Center at New Clark City with the BASIS Conversion

**[00:15:36.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000174)  
Development Authority.

**[00:15:37.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000175)  
Sa ating Prime HRM, which is a major program on human resource governance, the Prime HRM

**[00:15:48.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000176)  
aims to improve public sector HR systems, namely recruitment, selection, and placement,

**[00:15:55.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000178)  
placement, selection, and placement, career, performance management, learning and development,

**[00:15:56.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000179)  
and rewards and recognition.

**[00:15:57.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000180)  
477 HRM systems were recognized in 2024, increasing to 517 in 2025.

**[00:16:05.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000181)  
For the first semester of 2026, 193 HRM systems have already been recognized.

**[00:16:13.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000182)  
For accreditation, 134 agencies were accredited in 2024.

**[00:16:20.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000183)  
193 in 2020 and three years in between.

**[00:16:25.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000184)  
As of June 2026, 54 agencies have been accredited.

**[00:37:04.230] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000335)  
Support operations, bumaba po ito sa operations, 36.6 million increase due to inflationary adjustments of MOOE. Locally funded project from 0.774 million increase in LFP or local funded projects is also inflationary pressures. For foreign-assisted projects, because of the focus on the Philippine Civil Service Modernization Program.

**[00:37:34.210] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000336)  
So ulit po, in summary, in 2026, 4.18 ang hiningi natin. Ang binigay sa atin ay 2.89. Ngayon po, nadagdagan siya ng 1.145 billion attributable to; meron tayong 1.14 billion sa general subsidy, regular subsidy, at 1.45 billion

**[00:38:04.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000337)  
attributable

**[00:38:05.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000338)  
to the

**[00:38:06.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000339)  
PCSMP.

**[00:38:07.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000340)  
We

**[00:38:12.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000341)  
will now give you the

**[00:38:13.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000342)  
highlights of

**[00:38:14.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000343)  
our 2027 targets.

**[00:38:15.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000344)  
For 2027,

**[00:38:16.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000345)  
CSC

**[00:38:17.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000346)  
CSC will pursue digital transformation targets. These are development, initial implementation

**[00:38:24.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000347)  
and testing, transition, and capability building commitments in digital education.

**[00:38:33.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000348)  
The prime ESG will complete its assessment of the human resource management of target

**[00:38:39.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000349)  
agencies and complete their transition readiness for transition to the HRMIS plus PS system.

**[00:38:49.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000350)  
Sa HRMIS PS or Human Resource Management Information System plus Pre-Role System initiative, mag

**[00:38:59.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000351)  
go-live na po kami at the earliest, the last quarter ng 2026, but full go-live and testing

**[00:39:08.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000352)  
and customization.

**[00:39:09.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000353)  
Sa digital capability development, we have to provide role-based digital training for

**[00:39:20.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000354)  
CSC personnel, HRMO, system operators, maintainers, and support teams.

**[00:39:26.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000355)  
The point is simple.

**[00:39:28.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000356)  
The systems will only work if the people operating and maintaining them are prepared or digitally

**[00:39:34.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000357)  
enabled.

**[00:39:36.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000358)  
And we also aim to establish an integrated system.

**[00:39:38.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000359)  
Thank you.

**[00:39:39.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000360)  
Thank you.

**[00:39:40.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000361)  
Thank you for your questions.

**[00:39:41.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000362)  
So, first of all, we need to understand this system of identity, where it is located within

**[00:39:42.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000363)  
the system.

**[00:39:43.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000364)  
Secondly, we need to understand what is the local economy, where is the local social

**[00:39:44.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000365)  
system, so that it is a completely integrated electronic administrative system, so that

**[00:39:45.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000366)  
it will cover procurement, accounting, inventory, asset monitoring and management, to strengthen

**[00:39:51.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000367)  
internal processing, transparency, and audit readiness.

**[00:39:57.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000368)  
For professionalization and workplace cooperation, we are also in the process of developing a

**[00:40:04.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000369)  
national learning management system.

**[00:40:06.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000370)  
The minimum target for 2027 is the national digital learning management system.

**[00:40:06.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000371)  
We need to understand how these processes are going to affect the quality of the local

**[00:40:07.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000372)  
economy, especially in rural areas.

**[00:40:08.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000373)  
Next slide.

**[00:40:09.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000374)  
is to migrate at least 20 existing CSC e-learning courses into an operational national learning platform

**[00:40:17.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000375)  
to enable accessible, personalized, and scalable learning across the civil service.

**[00:40:24.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000376)  
We hope also to develop a secure, continuous online registration, examination, and eligibility certification system.

**[00:40:33.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000377)  
Ang ibig lang pong sabihin, ang aming vision dito ay magiging end-to-end ang system,

**[00:40:38.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000378)  
mag-register online sa pag-exam, ang mag-exam yung applicant,

**[00:40:45.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000379)  
and then ma-compute ang kanilang grades, ma-issue ang kanilang whether they passed or not,

**[00:40:52.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000380)  
and issue the certificate eligibility through one seamless process end-to-end.

**[00:40:58.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000381)  
Okay.

**[00:40:59.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000382)  
As to the accreditation and registration of public sector employees organization,

**[00:41:05.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000383)  
we also want to develop and accredit.

**[00:41:08.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000384)  
We want to develop an accreditation and registration electronic system

**[00:41:11.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000385)  
para mas madali ang mag-apply for registration and accreditation.

**[00:41:17.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000386)  
We are also intending to install a special eligibility through special law system.

**[00:41:24.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000387)  
Ibig sabihin, online na rin at digital ang ating pag-process ng applications at issuance ng special eligibilities

**[00:41:32.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000388)  
that are gained through special laws and issuances.

**[00:41:38.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000389)  
Sa administrative justice system, ito po ang pinakamalaking pangarap namin kasi mabagal po ang ating paper-based na administrative justice system.

**[00:41:50.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000390)  
The CSC Legal and Administrative Services System is a digital platform designed to modernize and streamline

**[00:41:57.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000391)  
the Civil Service Commission's legal and administrative service processes,

**[00:42:01.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000392)  
which means that there will be key services including electronic filing and service of

**[00:42:08.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000393)  
pleadings, motions, and other submissions for greater convenience and accessibility to clients nationwide.

**[00:42:15.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000394)  
There will be also electronic processing of CSC clearances para hindi na lang paper, document,

**[00:42:22.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000395)  
na papunta-punta pa balik-balik sa field offices at saka sa central office.

**[00:42:27.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000396)  
We will also include an online payment of applicable fees and charges and paperless transactions and real-time case status monitoring.

**[00:42:39.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000397)  
This is more or less an overall comprehensive overview of our targets for 2027.

**[00:42:48.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000398)  
Kahit marami po ang aming request for 2027, meron pa rin po kami yung mga unfunded proposals,

**[00:42:55.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000399)  
our wish list, so-called, kasi kapag nangailangan din po ito ng Civil Service Commission.

**[00:43:00.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000400)  
Ang nangunguna po dito ay ang mga infrastructure projects ng ating mga regional and field offices.

**[00:43:07.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000401)  
Pangpumapangalawa po dito ay operations.

**[00:43:09.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000402)  
Siyang subyak rin ang mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga mga.

**[00:43:20.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000403)  
Uh, karamihan na age ng aming mga vehicles are seven to nine years.

**[00:43:26.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000404)  
So, ready na po kami for, for, uh, refleeting.

**[00:43:31.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000405)  
And then the last one is, um, green energy.

**[00:43:35.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000406)  
Ang puntu lang po nito ay kailangan na po nating para...

**[00:43:39.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000407)  
mabawasan ang ating utilities, electric utilities budget, at para rin makasiguro po tayo na

**[00:43:47.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000408)  
merong backup energy sources dahil ang PCSMP, pag mag-digital transformation pa tayo, parami

**[00:43:54.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000409)  
po tayong online or electronic na systems, kailangan po ng mga backup na energy sources

**[00:44:00.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000410)  
para kahit mag-brown out ay meron po tayong, hindi po madi-disrupt ang ating mga systems.

**[00:44:06.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000411)  
All right. Just briefly, ito po yung first priority. Ito po kasi yung listahan ng mga regional

**[00:44:14.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000412)  
field offices na yung kanilang mga infrastructure needs ay ready for implementation, meaning

**[00:44:19.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000413)  
natapos na po nila yung pag-survey, pag-prepare ng plano. Ang kulang na lang po ay magkaroon

**[00:44:27.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000414)  
po sila ng grant ng pera para maipatayo itong mga field offices na ito kasama na ang mga

**[00:44:32.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000415)  
regional offices. Nakagaya rin po.

**[00:44:36.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000416)  
Ang pangalawa po ay yung pinatawag nating infrastructure improvement requirements.

**[00:44:46.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000417)  
Karamihan ay renovation and improvement of existing facilities such as records storage

**[00:44:53.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000418)  
and then dormitories, parking areas, and other operational facilities.

**[00:45:00.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000419)  
Ang pinakahuli sa lahat ng infrastructure needs ay mga tinatawag na building refurbishment, repainting, waterworks, and other necessary facility enhancements in our regional and field offices.

**[00:45:15.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000420)  
Sa next slide po, ito yung mga requirements natin sa operational mobility. Isang car for BARM and then yung tatlong units ng minibus for the central office kasi ito yung ginagamit for shuttle at saka for transporting people when they go to trainings, workshops, and other activities.

**[00:45:37.800] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000421)  
And yung passenger van, tatlo ang para sa central office at apat sa selected na regional offices.

**[00:45:45.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000422)  
And then the last one is the installation of a solar panel system and generator system. As I said earlier, ito ay green energy program.

**[00:45:59.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000423)  
The project will help reduce electricity costs, provide backup power for critical frontline and ICT services, and support the government's energy efficiency and green workplace policies.

**[00:46:16.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000424)  
Ngayong taon po, ngayong buwan ng September,

**[00:46:19.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000425)  
ang 126 years anniversary of the Philippine Civil Service Commission as the Central Human Resource Agency of Government.

**[00:46:29.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000426)  
We hope that you can join us in many of our activities sa anniversary.

**[00:46:34.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000427)  
I think ang pinakasikatwa yata yung mga discounts sa mga department stores kasamang aming mga partner agencies.

**[00:46:40.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000428)  
Meron po tayong fun run. Medyo na mukhang napuno natin ngayon ang slots for our fun run.

**[00:46:47.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000429)  
Ang bibigyan po ng proses.

**[00:46:49.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000430)  
Ang mga proceeds ng fun run, yung mga anak at saka mga byuda ng mga outstanding achievers in the civil service.

**[00:46:58.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000431)  
Ang tawag po dito ay gawad pamana.

**[00:47:02.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000432)  
Meron din po tayong iba pang mga, meron po tayong nationwide oratorical contest, essay writing contest, and a choral competition.

**[00:47:15.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000433)  
Yung oratorical at saka yung ating essay writing contest.

**[00:47:19.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000434)  
It is a means to encourage more young people to be informed about the Civil Service Commission.

**[00:47:25.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000435)  
At ang kanilang topic po ay puso, dangal, at galing para sa bayan.

**[00:47:30.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000436)  
It will culminate in, well we hope it will happen in September, pero because of the weather it might be postponed again.

**[00:47:38.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000437)  
Ito po ay ang gawad lingkod bayani, which recognizes as outstanding achievements both in the regional levels and in the national level,

**[00:47:47.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000438)  
which will be the gawad.

**[00:47:49.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000439)  
Ang lahat po ng aming mga programa ay pagsusulong ng aming layunin na gawing lingkod bayani ang bawat kawani na may puso, dangal, at galing para sa bayan.

**[00:48:07.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000440)  
Maraming salamat po and thank you for your attention.

**[00:48:26.810] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000443)

**[00:48:40.130] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000445)

**[00:48:42.850] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000446)

**[00:48:44.970] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000447)  
I would like also to acknowledge my co-sponsor, Deputy Majority Leader, Vice Chair,

**[00:48:56.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000448)

**[00:49:17.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000449)  
my favorite party member, Representative Eli San Fernando, Vice Chair...

**[00:49:32.130] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000450)

**[00:49:54.900] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000452)  
including the responses of the resource persons.

**[00:50:01.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000453)  
When the bell sounds, the members...

**[00:50:05.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000454)  
Has 30 seconds to conclude.

**[00:50:10.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000455)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House,

**[00:50:16.440] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000456)  
the Chair shall recognize, first, the minority,

**[00:50:19.780] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000457)

**[00:50:25.020] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000458)  
in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:50:33.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000459)  
The Chair first recognize...

**[00:50:35.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000460)  
The Honorable Praminority Representative, my boss, Robert Nazal.

**[00:50:43.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000461)  
Thank you to Mr. Chair.

**[00:50:48.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000462)  
Thank you to my idol, Congressman Bong.

**[00:50:54.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000463)  
Mr. Chair, through the Chair, Madam Secretary,

**[00:51:02.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000464)  
Madam Secretary,

**[00:51:02.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000465)  
we have held several budget hearings, and we are bringing up the seven-day parental leave

**[00:51:10.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000466)  
for qualified J.O. and C.O.S. solo parents.

**[00:51:17.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000467)  
Republic 11861 and its IRR have been in place since 2022.

**[00:51:24.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000468)  
Last year, Mr. Chair, CSC told us it would discuss this with DBM,

**[00:51:32.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000469)  
but in 2025, the CSC, COA, and DBM already issued a joint circular,

**[00:51:44.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000470)  
specifically governing J.O., your job order at Contract of Service Workers,

**[00:51:50.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000471)  
about providing ancillary benefits.

**[00:51:53.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000472)  
So, you already talked, ma'am.

**[00:51:56.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000473)  
Why didn't you include the seven-day solo parent leave?

**[00:52:00.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000474)  
Um...

**[00:52:02.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000475)  
It's already in the Joint Memorandum Circular, a specific provision of it.

**[00:52:08.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000476)  
Section 11.2 states that all benefits are already granted in existing laws.

**[00:52:18.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000477)  
So, it's included in the parental law.

**[00:52:20.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000478)  
So, you don't need to mention it anymore?

**[00:52:22.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000479)  
Even if we don't need to mention it anymore, but as a policy in the Civil Service,

**[00:52:26.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000480)  
we interpret the Solo Parent Welfare Act as...

**[00:52:31.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000481)  
Um...

**[00:52:32.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000482)  
As...

**[00:52:32.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000483)  
As being able to grant yung seven-day wellness leave to solo parents,

**[00:52:38.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000484)  
whether they be male or female.

**[00:52:41.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000485)  
May seven-day wellness leave sila.

**[00:52:43.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000486)  
Nag-hukay po kami sa archives namin, kasi darawang taon pa lang po ako sa komisyon.

**[00:52:49.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000487)  
Meron na pala kaming na-issue na circular in 2004,

**[00:52:52.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000488)  
allowing parenta a solo parent leave of seven days.

**[00:52:57.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000489)  
Has this been...

**[00:52:59.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000490)  
Mr. Chair, has this been communicated already?

**[00:53:02.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000491)  
I think...

**[00:53:02.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000492)  
To the Depart...

**[00:53:03.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000493)  
Na-circularize na po yan sa lahat ng Departamento.

**[00:53:05.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000494)  
So, ano po?

**[00:53:05.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000495)  
Alam na po ng mga Departamento yan?

**[00:53:07.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000496)  
Alam na po nila.

**[00:53:08.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000497)  
I mean...

**[00:53:08.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000498)  
Naa-apply na po ba?

**[00:53:10.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000499)  
Meron lang po akong a little bit of caution, kasi sa Solo Parents Welfare Act,

**[00:53:16.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000500)  
ang principal na implementing authority ay ang DSWD.

**[00:53:23.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000501)  
So, meron po kaming ongoing ngayon na mga dialogues para kung pwede ay ilabas na nila,

**[00:53:31.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000502)  
because they are the...

**[00:53:32.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000503)  
The Primary Implementing Agency for the Solo Parents Welfare Act.

**[00:53:36.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000504)  
Para i-clarify na po nila itong already recognized by the Civil Service Commission,

**[00:53:42.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000505)  
ang seven-day parental leave for solo parents ay available na po as early as 2004 from our end.

**[00:53:50.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000506)  
Siguro nga po...

**[00:53:51.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000507)  
But we also recognize para ma-implement yung batas at saka kaayon din yung joint circular namin ng DBM

**[00:54:01.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000508)  
at saka ng COA.

**[00:54:05.490] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000509)  
If necessary, if it is still necessary, but which I think is already, baka hindi na,

**[00:54:11.250] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000510)  
pero pwede naman kaming mag-issue ng reiterative issuance and application of the seven-day wellness leave for solo parents.

**[00:54:21.130] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000511)  
Baka po mas maganda, kasi I think there are still a lot of departments who are in fear of implementing the law.

**[00:54:30.730] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000512)  
Opo.

**[00:54:31.050] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000513)  
Kasi po, natatakot sila.

**[00:54:33.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000514)  
Sa audit or disallowance, yung mga ganon.

**[00:54:36.430] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000515)  
So, mas mabuti na po sigurong i-reiterate natin.

**[00:54:39.390] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000516)  
Opo.

**[00:54:40.010] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000517)  
Although, palagi po namin ginagamit nga yung particular section na yon ng Joint Memorandum Circular

**[00:54:45.210] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000518)  
kasi kasali na po doon ang DBM, which has jurisdiction over JOs and COs, at kasama na rin po ang COA.

**[00:54:52.410] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000519)  
So, yung mga audit concerns natin ay na-address na rin po.

**[00:54:57.530] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000520)  
Perhaps it can be reiterated, itong particular section ito ng Joint Memorandum.

**[00:55:03.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000521)  
Sige po, para lang po malaman din natin.

**[00:55:05.990] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000522)  
Kasi marami pa pong departments ang siguro hindi pa rin na-implement.

**[00:55:09.430] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000523)  
Opo. Salamat. Opo. We will do that. Opo.

**[00:55:12.690] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000524)  
Madam, Mr. Chair, through the Chair, what is CSC completely doing about the large number of unfilled plantilla positions across government

**[00:55:27.050] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000525)  
while we continue to rely heavily on JO and COS workers?

**[00:55:33.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000526)  
Sandali lang po, ma'am.

**[00:55:34.790] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000527)  
CSC's own inventory already accounts 919,868 JO and COS workers.

**[00:55:44.330] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000528)  
What is CSC's government-wide plan to match qualified JOCOS workers with vacant plantilla positions,

**[00:55:55.250] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000529)  
help those who lack eligibility obtain it,

**[00:55:59.270] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000530)  
and reduce both vacancies and...

**[00:56:03.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000531)  
...independence on contractual arrangements?

**[00:56:05.850] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000532)  
Can you please give us measurable targets and a timeline, not just programs?

**[00:56:12.590] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000533)  
I am reluctant to give targets kasi parang ito yung absorption ng JOs and COs into the civil service.

**[00:56:22.570] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000534)  
Kasi po, ang appointing authority po ay hindi po kami, unang-una.

**[00:56:27.670] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000535)  
Pangalawa po, kaya hindi mo pwedeng isigurado,

**[00:56:31.990] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000536)  
kasi dapat yung JO at CO, yung kanyang qualifications, ay fit dun sa vacant na posisyon.

**[00:56:39.250] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000537)  
Kadalasan po yung mga nag-a-apply, hindi fit yung qualifications nila sa ina-applyan na posisyon.

**[00:56:45.350] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000538)  
However, I think yung pinaka-ano namin na program so far is the grant of preferential rating for JOs and COs.

**[00:56:57.710] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000539)  
Ang ibig sabihin, mag-exam sila.

**[00:57:01.990] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000540)  
And then, huwag lang bababa sa 70% ang kanilang grade, ang kanilang score.

**[00:57:08.210] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000541)  
Magdadagdaga ng 10% para makapasa po sila.

**[00:57:12.250] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000542)  
So, mas maluwag na po yung avenue to enter government service.

**[00:57:17.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000543)  
But their passing of the civil service eligibility exam,

**[00:57:21.090] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000544)  
kailangan pa rin po nilang mag-comply sa other qualification requirements such as experience,

**[00:57:26.910] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000545)  
training, educational qualifications.

**[00:57:31.990] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000546)  
Pero ang idinagdag namin para mas mapaluwag ang daanan ng JOs at COs,

**[00:57:38.210] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000547)  
dati 10 years experience ang hinihingi namin sa kanila bago sila makapag-apply sa CS preferential rating.

**[00:57:45.450] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000548)  
Ngayon, hinati na po namin 5 years na lang.

**[00:57:49.790] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000549)  
So, kung matagal na siya sa government service, yung kanyang experience requirement fulfilled na rin niya.

**[00:57:55.650] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000550)  
Ang problema na lang niya is yung training hours niya, training or competency hours niya,

**[00:58:00.970] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000551)  
at saka yung kanyang educational qualification.

**[00:58:04.490] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000552)  
But if they have educational qualifications fit for first level or second level,

**[00:58:11.650] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000553)  
then they can get in the government service.

**[00:58:14.010] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000554)  
When did you implement this change?

**[00:58:17.110] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000555)  
Itong 5 years? Is it the 5 years?

**[00:58:19.550] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000556)  
Yes.

**[00:58:20.550] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000557)  
Early this year.

**[00:58:21.930] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000558)  
Early this year.

**[00:58:22.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000559)  
After nung matapos ang budget deliberation.

**[00:58:26.330] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000560)  
So, after the budget deliberation.

**[00:58:27.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000561)  
Ano po yung naging epekto niya?

**[00:58:29.850] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000562)  
Ilan po ang na-fix?

**[00:58:30.970] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000563)  
Ano po yung na-fill na plantilya?

**[00:58:33.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000564)  
Mahirap pong i-monitor ng ganon yung ilan ang ma-fill na dahil pinaluwag.

**[00:58:39.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000565)  
Pero merong increase in the number of applicants and passers sa CSPR.

**[00:58:46.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000566)  
I cannot at this point in time, kasi wala akong datos na hawak,

**[00:58:50.210] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000567)  
establish a direct correlation between those measures

**[00:58:56.710] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000568)  
and the absorption of JOs and COOs in the system.

**[00:59:00.970] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000569)  
The only thing I can say is we will keep working at making absorption in the civil service

**[00:59:08.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000570)  
more efficient and more accessible.

**[00:59:12.490] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000571)  
So, if we are talking about numbers, ilang percent po ang binaba or ang na-absorb sa plantilya from JOs and COOs?

**[00:59:26.110] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000572)  
Kasi, di ba, matagal na yung problema to eh.

**[00:59:29.090] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000573)  
May mga JOs tayo.

**[00:59:30.970] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000574)  
May mga COOs na more than 10 years na hung nagtatrabaho.

**[00:59:34.750] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000575)  
May mga 15 years na na nagtatrabaho, JO pa rin.

**[00:59:38.790] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000576)  
Ano po bang, may pag-asa po po pa ho ba silang ma-plantilya?

**[00:59:44.690] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000577)  
Kaya nga po, pag ma-fulfill po nila yung requirement.

**[00:59:48.890] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000578)  
Kasi hindi po naman namin pwedeng tanggalin yung requirement ng educational achievement,

**[00:59:54.290] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000579)  
yung experience, training, at saka appropriate civil service eligibility.

**[01:00:00.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000580)  
kasi nandito po sa EO 292

**[01:00:02.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000581)  
yun. Legislation po ito

**[01:00:04.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000582)  
at hindi po kami ang pwedeng magpalit

**[01:00:06.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000583)  
niyan. Nandun na po tayo eh. Assuming

**[01:00:08.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000584)  
sila, marami naman hong

**[01:00:09.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000585)  
pasado eh. Pero let's call

**[01:00:12.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000586)  
a spade a spade. Minsan po o kadalasan

**[01:00:14.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000587)  
ang nangyayari po talaga dito,

**[01:00:15.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000588)  
palakasan o ayaw talagang punuin.

**[01:00:18.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000589)  
Diba? Ayaw punuin yung plantilya.

**[01:00:21.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000590)  
So,

**[01:00:21.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000591)  
what will the CSC do

**[01:00:24.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000592)  
concretely

**[01:00:25.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000593)  
para po maiwasan na po

**[01:00:28.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000594)  
ito? Kasi

**[01:00:28.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000595)  
all the departments, ganyan po ang issue natin.

**[01:00:32.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000596)  
Ang dami nung plantilya

**[01:00:33.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000597)  
positions, pero hindi naman

**[01:00:36.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000598)  
siya napupuno. Pero

**[01:00:37.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000599)  
despite that, napakadami ng JOs

**[01:00:40.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000600)  
and COs. Hindi naman

**[01:00:41.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000601)  
pwede natin sabihin na walang qualified doon.

**[01:00:44.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000602)  
Ilan taon na nagtatrabaho

**[01:00:45.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000603)  
doon. I appreciate

**[01:00:48.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000604)  
yung concern po ninyo.

**[01:00:49.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000605)  
Ang concern ko po naman

**[01:00:51.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000606)  
ay wala pong datos to establish.

**[01:00:53.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000607)  
A direct correlation

**[01:00:54.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000608)  
between yung mga nakakakuha

**[01:00:58.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000609)  
ng

**[01:00:58.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000610)  
CSPR

**[01:00:59.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000611)  
at saka yung absorption in the

**[01:01:02.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000612)  
civil service.

**[01:01:04.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000613)  
Subukan po natin as a

**[01:01:06.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000614)  
continuing concern, baka pag pumasok

**[01:01:08.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000615)  
na po yung system ng CSEVs,

**[01:01:10.300] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000616)  
ay ng data management system,

**[01:01:12.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000617)  
baka may maghugot po tayo dito

**[01:01:14.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000618)  
na datos para maka-establish

**[01:01:16.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000619)  
ng correlation or prepare a program

**[01:01:18.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000620)  
in terms of absorption.

**[01:01:21.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000621)  
One thing really

**[01:01:22.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000622)  
is we cannot guarantee

**[01:01:23.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000623)  
employment in the civil service

**[01:01:27.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000624)  
habang

**[01:01:28.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000625)  
nandoon yung mga

**[01:01:30.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000626)  
qualification requirements,

**[01:01:32.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000627)  
habang may nirequire na

**[01:01:34.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000628)  
qualification standards,

**[01:01:36.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000629)  
nakatali po ang ating kamay,

**[01:01:38.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000630)  
kailangan po natin i-implement yun.

**[01:01:40.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000631)  
I would suggest

**[01:01:42.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000632)  
siguro is to review

**[01:01:44.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000633)  
the laws affecting JOs and COs

**[01:01:46.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000634)  
because as a general

**[01:01:48.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000635)  
rule, we are not

**[01:01:50.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000636)  
empowered as a commission

**[01:01:51.820] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000637)  
to take up

**[01:01:53.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000638)  
yung

**[01:01:54.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000639)  
employment of JOs and COs.

**[01:01:58.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000640)  
Ito po ay nasa

**[01:02:00.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000641)  
poder ng DBM, but

**[01:02:01.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000642)  
it's something that legislation

**[01:02:03.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000643)  
may cure.

**[01:02:07.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000644)  
CSE Civil Service Professionalization

**[01:02:09.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000645)  
and Workplace Cooperation Program

**[01:02:11.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000646)  
is proposed

**[01:02:13.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000647)  
at P725.7

**[01:02:16.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000648)  
million for 2027.

**[01:02:18.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000649)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:02:19.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000650)  
Madam Chair?

**[01:02:21.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000651)  
Mr. Chair?

**[01:02:23.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000652)  
I-check po lang po.

**[01:02:26.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000653)  
Ano yung

**[01:02:27.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000654)  
professionalization?

**[01:02:29.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000655)  
Ang tanong ko po doon ay

**[01:02:34.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000656)  
what measurable reduction in

**[01:02:36.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000657)  
vacancies, hiring time, and reliance

**[01:02:39.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000658)  
and JOCOs

**[01:02:41.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000659)  
Congress should expect

**[01:02:43.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000660)  
from this pending?

**[01:02:47.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000661)  
CSE's own

**[01:02:48.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000662)  
fiscal year 2027 budget

**[01:02:51.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000663)  
notes show

**[01:02:53.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000664)  
202 unfilled

**[01:02:54.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000665)  
CSE proper positions

**[01:02:56.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000666)  
and 28 unfilled CSB positions.

**[01:03:00.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000667)  
Can I get the question

**[01:03:06.090] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000668)  
again? Bakit?

**[01:03:07.850] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000669)  
What measurable reduction in vacancies, hiring time, and reliance

**[01:03:12.150] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000670)  
on JOs and COs

**[01:03:14.950] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000671)  
should Congress expect

**[01:03:16.170] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000672)  
from this pending?

**[01:03:18.670] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000673)  
Please forgive me, sir.

**[01:03:20.350] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000674)  
I really do not have

**[01:03:22.250] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000675)  
at this point in time

**[01:03:23.950] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000676)  
indicators to establish

**[01:03:26.170] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000677)  
a correlation

**[01:03:26.970] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000678)  
ng JOs

**[01:03:30.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000679)  
and COs

**[01:03:31.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000680)  
tapos papunta kung ilan sa kanila

**[01:03:33.890] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000681)  
ang na-absorb sa gobyerno.

**[01:03:35.850] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000682)  
Baka po, it's about time that we should

**[01:03:37.730] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000683)  
have these indicators and...

**[01:03:39.310] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000684)  
Wala po tayong tracing ng JOs and COs.

**[01:03:42.150] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000685)  
Precisely po, wala po tayong roster

**[01:03:44.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000686)  
ng JOs and COs

**[01:03:45.890] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000687)  
kasi sila hindi kasali sa

**[01:03:47.730] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000688)  
civil service na jurisdiction.

**[01:03:50.810] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000689)  
Kasi under

**[01:14:24.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000904)  
bang pondo para sa

**[01:14:25.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000905)  
monitoring, enforcement,

**[01:14:27.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000906)  
and awareness campaigns,

**[01:14:30.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000907)  
kaugnay ng gambling ban

**[01:14:31.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000908)  
at paano ito isinama

**[01:14:33.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000909)  
sa budget proposal ng Civil Service?

**[01:14:37.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000910)  
Naglagay ba tayo ng...

**[01:14:39.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000911)  
Hindi.

**[01:14:42.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000912)  
I-cite mo yung...

**[01:14:46.780] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000913)  
May I direct

**[01:14:48.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000914)  
Assistant Commissioner

**[01:14:50.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000915)  
Ariel Ronquillo who drafted

**[01:14:51.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000916)  
the resolution. Pinapasa yung

**[01:14:54.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000917)  
blame mo. Kaya para

**[01:14:56.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000918)  
siya ang gusto po ang sumagot.

**[01:14:57.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000919)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:15:00.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000920)  
Madam Chair, Congresswoman Ismula. Sa resolution po, meron pong provision on reporting and enforcement. It's letter D of the resolution. Nakalagay po dito, any official, employee, client, or concerned member of the public may report violations of this policy through the CSE Contact Center ng Bayan or other complaint referral system.

**[01:15:26.700] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000921)  
And reports may also be made anonymously and shall be acted upon promptly in accordance with existing laws, rules, and regulations. So yun po yung means of monitoring.

**[01:15:38.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000922)  
Kasama ito sa current na budget din ng Civil Service, yung monitoring po, tama?

**[01:15:44.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000923)  
Yes, because Contact Center ng Bayan and Public Assistance Complaints Desk are installed in all regional and field offices.

**[01:15:52.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000924)  
Okay. Paano po natin ito naisama yung budget sa proposal ng Civil Service? Can you clarify lang po?

**[01:16:06.840] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000925)  
Madam Chair, yung pong pagbigay po ng budget sa aming Contact Center ng Bayan po, kasi one of the ways we monitor is through complaints that will be received by our Contact Center ng Bayan.

**[01:16:20.160] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000926)  
At yung pong Contact Center ng Bayan po ay kasama po sa programa ng komisyon.

**[01:16:25.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000927)  
May mga naitala na po bang mga disciplinary actions laban sa mga lumabag dito?

**[01:16:35.100] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000928)  
Ah, dito po?

**[01:16:35.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000929)  
Yes po.

**[01:16:36.480] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000930)  
Ah, dito po, kasi ito po ay relatively new. Wala pa po. Pero in the past, pertaining to the previous policy on prohibition on gambling, meron pong mga cases po doon.

**[01:16:49.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000931)  
Okay. So for the new resolution, yung 2026, wala pa?

**[01:16:53.260] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000932)  
Wala pa po, based on our records.

**[01:16:55.100] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000933)  
Okay.

**[01:16:55.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000934)  
Ah, masasabi niyo po ba na may efekto na kaya itong resolution na ito sa kultura at integridad ng disiplina at servisyong pampubliko?

**[01:17:06.240] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000935)  
Madam Chair, masasabi ko po na may epekto na po kasi in a number of times, nakakatanggap po kami ng mga tanong at mga comments regarding this.

**[01:17:22.880] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000936)  
At we've been telling them na ang purpose po is to maintain the right conduct of our

**[01:17:28.960] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000937)  
government officials and employees, because they are considered as role models in society.

**[01:17:35.220] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000938)  
At nag-iingat naman po sila, sinasabi po nila sa amin, buti na lang na paliwanagan niyo kami, so we know how to act accordingly.

**[01:17:44.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000939)  
Okay. Just a follow-up on that din, would you also support a law banning all government employees from engaging in online gambling?

**[01:17:56.260] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000940)  
Madam Chair, sa totoo.

**[01:17:58.960] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-000941)  
Sa totoo lang po, kasama na po siya dito, kasi yung gambling po na pinoprohibit natin ay on-site pati po online.

**[01:18:05.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000942)  
Okay. So ibig sabihin, even without the bill enacted or approved, dyan sa resolusyon iyan, sumasang-ayon na po kayo.

**[01:18:15.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000943)  
Okay. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:18:17.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000944)  
Ako'y dadako naman sa another topic, ibang katanungan naman po.

**[01:18:23.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000945)  
Sa IPMR, sub-professional or first-level eligibility.

**[01:18:28.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000946)  
Ito'y patungkol po sa ating mga indigenous people mandatory representative.

**[01:18:36.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000947)  
Sa ilalim po kasi ng Indigenous Peoples Rights Act or yung RA 8371, may mandato na magkaroon ng indigenous people mandatory representative sa mga sangguniang level, sa barangay, municipal, and even in the provincial level.

**[01:18:55.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000948)  
As regards to this, the civil

**[01:18:58.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000949)  
Service eligibility of IPMRs, gusto ko lang i-clarify kung ano po yung mga umiiral na pulisiya ng civil service hinggil dito, at meron ba itong pagkakaiba sa eligibility ng elected councillors?

**[01:19:21.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000950)  
Anong pagkakaiba sa eligibility ng elected councillors po?

**[01:19:25.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000951)  
May pagkakaiba po ba sila?

**[01:19:26.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000952)  
Sa mga elected kasi na councillors, members ng sanggunian ito, specific law yung nag-provide sa kanila.

**[01:19:34.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000953)  
Nag-grant ng eligibility.

**[01:19:36.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000954)  
Kaya nga po madalas kaming makatanggap, nabigyan na yung mga barangay, pati yung elected officials ng barangay, appointed officials ng barangay, pinigyan na sila ng eligibility under the law.

**[01:19:49.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000955)  
Nabigyan na rin yung mga lahat ng members ng sanggunian.

**[01:19:52.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000956)  
Pero yung mayor at saka yung governor, wala.

**[01:19:54.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000957)  
Wala po kasi dun sa batas na sila ay mapasali.

**[01:19:58.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000958)  
In regards naman po sa indigenous peoples mandatory IPMRs,

**[01:20:04.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000959)  
eligibility, wala pa po kami ngayon.

**[01:20:10.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000960)  
But last budget hearing, one senator requested that if we could study the possibility of granting preferential treatment for indigenous peoples as a class.

**[01:20:22.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000961)  
Medyo nahirapan po kami doon, not only dahil nag-object ang indigenous, most representatives of indigenous peoples from Luzon.

**[01:20:31.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000962)  
Pero we asked our field offices in Visayas and Mindanao.

**[01:20:34.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000963)  
To consult with their IPs kung pwede.

**[01:20:38.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000964)  
Although I recognize the difficulty because parang bibigyan mo sila ng preferential rating,

**[01:20:45.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000965)  
dahil lang sa indigenous peoples sila parang medyo kailangan may some kind of qualifying standard.

**[01:20:54.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000966)  
But if they are kagaya ng at par sa condition ng nag-represent sa barangay council, sa sanggunian,

**[01:21:01.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000967)  
and other key government offices.

**[01:21:04.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000968)  
Mandatory representative nga sila.

**[01:21:06.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000969)  
I think we can work at issuing a preferential rating system for IPMRs.

**[01:21:14.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000970)  
For the meantime.

**[01:21:15.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000971)  
Yeah, I think we can do it.

**[01:21:18.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000972)  
We have three months before the year ends.

**[01:21:23.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000973)  
Can I, can you?

**[01:21:25.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-000974)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:21:25.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000975)  
Can you?

**[01:21:27.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000976)  
Yes, I think we'll.

**[01:21:29.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000977)  
Pero pwersahan na po yata ito.

**[01:21:31.380] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000978)  
Sorry.

**[01:21:31.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000979)  
But meron po tayong basis ngayon.

**[01:21:34.520] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000980)  
Kasi nagsilbi sila sa pamahalan.

**[01:21:37.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000981)  
Mandatory representative sila sa key government agencies.

**[01:21:40.440] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-000982)  
So we can probably extend the coverage of the civil service preferential rating eligibility for IPMRs.

**[01:21:48.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000983)  
Magandang hakbang po ito, no?

**[01:22:06.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000987)

**[01:22:20.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000988)  
Sila'y mga lingkod bayan.

**[01:22:22.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000989)

**[01:22:27.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000990)  
So magandang hakbang po ito, Madam Chair,

Kami po sa Akbayan Reform

ng QS.

central office.

**[01:29:46.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001202)  
Ano?

Ma'am, mga isang buwan na po yun

**[01:29:51.280] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001204)  
na-submit nila.

**[01:30:00.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001208)  
Okay, okay. Yeah, yeah. Yeah, we will trace the documents and then look at them accordingly and we will get back to you. I will ask Ariel, ASCOM Ariel, to directly coordinate with you.

**[01:30:13.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001209)  
Pero ang sa akin po kasi, from sa bare na information na ibinigay ninyo po sa akin, ang sinabi ninyo, mas mataas kaysa QS na binibigay ng CSC. Ang CSC is minimum qualification requirements.

**[01:30:31.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001210)  
Agencies have the general power and authority to put it into a higher level of qualification standards.

**[01:30:38.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001211)  
So, from that general principle, baka ho natin

**[01:30:43.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001212)  
mapag-aralan at maisaayos yung request ninyo as soonest possible time.

**[01:30:48.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001212-split-2)  
That's the reason po. Nangangailangan sila ng inyong approval whether to adopt or not.

**[01:30:54.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001213)  
Dahil they are anticipating later on mga legal actions.

**[01:30:58.440] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001214)  
Kung halimbawa, right away they will adopt the DOH dahil meron ho kayong guidelines din.

**[01:31:03.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001215)  
Pero kung may express approval na po ng civil service, I think medyo safe na sila sa kanilang pagpili ng bagong mga physicians.

**[01:31:12.100] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001216)  
We will act po immediately on that request.
