**[01:31:19.860] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001220)  
Thank you so much, Rep. Momo.

**[01:31:22.140] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001221)  
Next to Interpelate, Representative Elijah John Fernando.

**[01:31:28.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001222)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[01:31:30.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001223)  
Dito po sa report ninyo, Madam Chairperson, dumami ang bilang ng mga JO at COS sa National Government Agencies.

**[01:31:38.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001224)  
Ano po ang mga ahensya na may pinakamaraming bilang ng JOs at COS?

**[01:31:43.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001225)  
Meron tayo?

**[01:31:46.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001226)  
Kahit yung top five, top ten.

**[01:31:48.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001227)  
Ikaw, ikaw.

**[01:31:49.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001228)  
Ikaw.

**[01:31:50.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001229)  
Can I ask him?

**[01:31:51.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001230)  
Kasi siya ang may hawak ng datos dito.

**[01:31:54.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001231)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:31:56.020] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001232)  
The national government agencies with the highest number of JOs are the DPWH, DPWH, DOH, DSWD,

**[01:32:09.820] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001233)  
and yeah, that's the four highest.

**[01:32:15.040] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001234)  
This is your question?

**[01:32:15.180] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001235)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:32:15.200] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001236)  
Ang common feature nila, Madam Chair, is that sila yung namimigay ng mga welfare benefits.

**[01:32:25.020] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001237)  
Kaya seasonal at episodic minsan yung mga ibang mga pangangailangan nila.

**[01:32:29.400] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001238)  
That's what we can attribute yung pagdami ng number of JOs sa kanila.

**[01:32:37.140] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001239)  
To be precise, Madam Chair, DPWH ang pinakamarami at 44,870, Department of Health at 35,183, Department of Education at 35,014, Department of Social Welfare and Development at 23,819, and rounding up the top five is DENR at 12,840.

**[01:32:58.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001240)  
Makihingi na lang po kami ng copy, Madam Chairperson.

**[01:33:03.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001241)  
Marami na pong mga issuances na nilabas.

**[01:33:05.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001242)  
Ang CSC at DBM na sinasabi, ang mga job order at contract of service ay dapat hindi gagampan ng trabaho gaya ng isang regular na empleyado.

**[01:33:19.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001243)  
Tama po ba to?

**[01:33:21.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001244)  
Tama po.

**[01:33:22.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001245)  
Okay po.

**[01:33:23.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001246)  
Pero nakakapagtaka po, Madam Chair, sa mismong datos ng gobyerno, napakarami ng bilang ng mga job order at contract of service

**[01:33:32.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001247)  
na nagtatrabaho ng mahigit anim na taon o higit pa.

**[01:33:35.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001248)  
Ibig sabihin nito, Madam Chair,

**[01:33:41.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001249)  
eh, kumbaga, kung nasa pribadong sektor lang ho tayo,

**[01:33:45.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001250)  
numero unong violator, last year, ito rin po sinabi ko eh,

**[01:33:48.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001251)  
numero unong violator ang gobyerno ng labor code, kung nasa pribadong sektor lang tayo.

**[01:33:54.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001252)  
Kasi usually necessary and desirable na itong mga to eh.

**[01:33:57.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001253)  
Kasi paulit-ulit lang naman sila,

**[01:33:59.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001254)  
na hinahire ng mga job order at contract of service,

**[01:34:03.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001255)  
doing the same regular job,

**[01:34:05.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001256)  
all over again, sa iba't-ibang ahensya ng gobyerno.

**[01:34:08.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001257)  
So, mismong datos na ng CSA, nagsasabi nito.

**[01:34:11.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001258)  
Ngayon, ang tanong ko, Madam Chairperson, eh,

**[01:34:13.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001259)  
kung ganito po ang kalakaran sa National Government Agency, sa gobyerno mismo,

**[01:34:18.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001260)  
ano yung mga corrective measure or action na ginagawa ng Civil Service Commission?

**[01:34:26.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001261)  
Madam Chair, may I be allowed to have a little latitude in answering the question?

**[01:34:30.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001262)  
Kasi medyo complex po yung issue ng JOKO sa gobyerno natin.

**[01:34:34.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001263)  
Eh, eh, eh.

**[01:34:35.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001264)  
If the Honorable Ellie would grant that.

**[01:34:38.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001265)  
Ah, I share with the frustration of everyone,

**[01:34:40.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001266)  
like si Congressman Nazal kanina, tsaka si Congressman San Fernando.

**[01:34:44.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001267)  
And I agree that there are really employees who should have been made permanent by now,

**[01:34:49.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001268)  
because they've been engaged for work by their government agencies continuously for several years already,

**[01:34:54.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001269)  
leading one to conclude that their functions are regular and necessary.

**[01:34:59.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001270)  
But on the other hand, Madam Chair, I also personally believe that the COSJOs,

**[01:35:04.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001271)  
this unemployment arrangement, is still crucial for projects and programs that are temporary or episodic in nature,

**[01:35:11.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001272)  
such as welfare distribution activities and others.

**[01:35:14.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001273)  
This explains why among NGAs, DPWH, DOH, DSWD account for the most number of COSJOs.

**[01:35:23.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001274)  
It would be financially irresponsible to convert the second category to permanent positions because of the basic nature of their work,

**[01:35:32.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001275)  
which is to temporarily and seasonably distribute welfare benefits.

**[01:35:39.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001276)  
But I agree, Mr. Congressman, that the challenge for all of us is how to minimize,

**[01:35:45.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001277)  
if not eliminate, the first category of workers engaged as COSJOs.

**[01:35:49.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001278)  
Yung dinescribe niyo po na performing functions that are regular, desirable, and necessary for the particular agency.

**[01:35:59.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001279)  
While we do not have the exact answers that will guarantee the total elimination,

**[01:36:01.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001280)  
of COS and JOs in that first category, Madam Chair,

**[01:36:06.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001281)  
I believe the following can help address the situation,

**[01:36:09.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001282)  
some of which are my personal opinion.

**[01:36:11.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001283)  
Hindi po namin na pag-usapan pa sa CSC yung mga specific action points na ito.

**[01:36:16.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001284)  
Pero paalis na po ako sa komisyon, so baka may latitude po ako na magsabi ng mga sarili kong saluobin

**[01:36:22.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001285)  
kung ano dapat ang, ano tingin ko ang pwede nating mga measures na pwedeng i-adapt.

**[01:36:27.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001286)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[01:36:28.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001287)  
Number one po.

**[01:36:29.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001288)  
The DILG has two questions.

**[01:36:30.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001289)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[01:36:31.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001290)  
First, we need to conduct a scientific study to determine whether the rigid PS caps or PS limitations of LGUs remain relevant

**[01:36:38.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001291)  
and or whether a cap on the number of JOs and COS in LGUs must be implemented.

**[01:36:43.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001292)  
And they should coordinate with DBM on how to use this PS cap to administer or to regulate the number of COS and LGUs.

**[01:36:56.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001293)  
Each LGU has unique needs and unique capacities.

**[01:36:59.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001294)  
Alam po, suguro niyo yan.

**[01:37:00.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001295)  
This will be the greatest challenge po because out of the total number of JOs and COS in government, 70% po, 70% po ay nasa LGUs.

**[01:37:10.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001296)  
So makakatulong po dito kaya namin isinusulong yung sana magkaroon ng HRMO na professional, permanent mandatory positions sa bawat LGU

**[01:37:21.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001297)  
para ma-assist po ang mga LGUs in processing their recruitment, selection, and appointment needs.

**[01:37:27.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001298)  
Kasi baka some of these po ay caused by the government.

**[01:37:28.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001299)  
Baka some of these po ay caused by the fact na medyo matagal at nahihirapan yung LGUs to perform all the necessary recruitment,

**[01:37:36.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001300)  
selection, and appointment of workers which includes JOs and COS.

**[01:37:42.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001301)  
Number two po, baka malaki din po ang role ng Congress because I think we have to carefully review present laws

**[01:37:50.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001302)  
that create mandatory positions without regard to the needs and capacities of the particular LGUs.

**[01:37:55.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001303)  
While the intention in the creation of mandatory positions,

**[01:37:56.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001304)  
While the intention in the creation of mandatory positions,

**[01:37:57.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001305)  
While the intention in the creation of mandatory positions,

**[01:37:58.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001306)  
is noble po.

**[01:37:59.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001307)  
If done indiscriminately regardless of the capability of the LGUs,

**[01:38:03.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001308)  
it incapacitates po yung low-income LGUs who cannot afford to create these mandatory department head positions

**[01:38:10.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001309)  
para makapag-

**[01:38:11.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001310)  
kasi po pag nag-create ka ng mga bababawang posisyon pero hindi ka na-create ng mandatory posisyon,

**[01:38:16.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001311)  
bawal po yun at i-invalidate po namin yun.

**[01:38:19.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001312)  
So dahil may mga mandatory na matataas na posisyon na hindi kaya sa budget ng LGU na mag-create ng mga gantong posisyon,

**[01:38:25.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001313)  
hindi po nagagawan ng plantilya positions na hindi kaya sa budget ng LGUs na mag-create ng mga gantong posisyon,

**[01:38:26.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001314)  
hindi po nagagawan ng plantilya positions ng ibang LGUs yung mga utility nila na antagal nang nagtatrabaho sa kanila.

**[01:38:32.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001315)  
So baka kailangan po ng masinsinang pag-aaral dun sa mga batas.

**[01:38:35.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001316)  
And currently po on the part of the CSC tulad ng tanong ni Congressman Eli kanina on what we're doing,

**[01:38:40.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001317)  
may current discussion po kami, may nakasalang na matter on this particular subject matter

**[01:38:45.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001318)  
and hopefully within this year po makakapag-recommend kami ng way forward on how we can recommend to the Congress

**[01:38:55.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001319)  
kung ano yung mga posisyons na lagyan naman ng nuance kung mandatory or optional dapat siya

**[01:39:01.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001320)  
at hindi na lang basta i-dikta sa mga LGUs na mandatory siya kasi ang laki po ng impact sa mga LGUs

**[01:39:07.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001321)  
in their ability to hire or to create plantilya positions for their long-standing COS and JOs.

**[01:39:13.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001322)  
Number three na pwede pong i-consider natin na solusyon sa problema, at the risk of offending the DBM po,

**[01:39:20.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001323)  
I think it should relax its insistence on the scrap and build as a matter of default policy.

**[01:39:25.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001324)  
Many government agencies keep their obsolete positions vacant so that they have something to scrap when a need to create a position comes around.

**[01:39:34.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001325)  
Instead, I believe we should craft an honest-to-goodness policy where a government agency can create positions it

**[01:39:40.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001326)  
legitimately needs without having to find any position to scrap.

**[01:39:44.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001327)  
As a counterbalance naman, kasi naiintindihan naman natin kung saan nanggagaling ang DBM,

**[01:39:49.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001328)  
the offices in charge of optimization should expedite analyzing

**[01:39:55.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001329)  
longstanding vacant positions and pursue the removal in the government inventory of plantilla positions if found meritorious.

**[01:39:59.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001330)  
Kasi po kahit sinasight natin na ang daming vacancy kanina, ang katotohanan, some of it obsolete na kaya hindi nila pinupunan

**[01:40:05.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001331)  
pero nire-reserba nila para pag nangailangan sila ng ipapa-create na posisyon

**[01:40:08.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001332)  
meron silang i-offer sa DBM na i-scrap para makapag-build sila ng isa.

**[01:40:15.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001333)  
And finally, as an improvement sa ongoing po na work na ang partner po namin si Commissioner Louie ang in-charge on the part of CSC

**[01:40:22.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001334)  
pagdating sa committee on optimization.

**[01:40:25.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001335)  
I believe po, I submit po na dapat, we should not wait for an omnibus recommendation for the whole bureaucracy.

**[01:40:33.680] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001336)  
Masyado pong matagal at masyado pong maraming workers ang dadaanan nito.

**[01:40:37.820] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001337)  
Kaya kung sana per agency natin i-analyze yung staffing needs niya at i-implement na natin agad,

**[01:40:43.960] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001338)  
mas mabilis po yun dapat na mangyari.

**[01:40:46.100] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001339)  
These matters po ay on the demand side, on the supply side ng pagkontrol ng JOs and COS.

**[01:40:52.900] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001340)  
On the demand side naman po, I recognize po yung attractiveness on the part of agencies na mas madali kasi mag-hire ng JOs at COS.

**[01:41:00.880] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001341)  
Hindi siya dadaan sa masusing at masalimut na proseso, mas matagal siya.

**[01:41:06.560] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001342)  
E sa mandala ng JOs and COS, ambilis niya.

**[01:41:08.760] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001343)  
So siguro pwede siguro pong i-consider ng mga bumubuo ng optimization committee na part of their work is to explore the possibility na may mga trigger points po.

**[01:41:19.180] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001344)  
Na pag itong ahensya nito, parati na lang nag-hire.

**[01:41:22.400] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001345)  
Repeatedly hire COS and JOs for the same position for a number of years may trigger na automatic magkakaroon ng review

**[01:41:30.220] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001346)  
kung na ibibigay mo yung burden sa kanya to justify bakit patuloy na ginagawa niya ito para ma-shift po yung burden.

**[01:41:36.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001347)  
Ang CSA po, we join with your desire na mabawasan yung COS and JOs pero medyo tali po yung kamay namin

**[01:41:43.320] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001348)  
at yun lang po yung medyo limitado kaya namin i-offer on top of our policies na like yung preference rating nga po

**[01:41:49.980] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001349)  
para maibsan yung problema na may mga hindi nakakapasa eh kahit papano makatulong kami dito sa pag-solve ng problem.

**[01:41:56.760] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001350)  
But I agree, this is a very difficult problem, Kong. I agree with you. Masalimutang problema.

**[01:42:03.020] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001351)  
Pero may mga, tulad ng in-outline ko po, may mga four siguran na general concepts na pwede nating i-pursue to finally at least, at least man lang,

**[01:42:12.660] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001352)  
minimize yung prevalence po ng JOs, COS in the first category.

**[01:42:17.640] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001353)  
Kasi yung second category po na ginagamit siya for temporary and episodic, talagang importante po siya para maging agile ang ating gobyerno.

**[01:42:26.500] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001354)  
Na hindi po pwedeng i-permanent mo yan kasi may captured money ka dyan na sinusweldohan mo walang ginagawa.

**[01:42:33.120] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001355)  
So I still believe po na COS and JOs, when employed correctly, e malaking bagay in making sure na agile po yung gobyerno natin.

**[01:42:42.580] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001356)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[01:42:43.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001357)  
Thank you.

**[01:42:44.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001358)  
I agree with most of your observations.

**[01:42:49.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001359)  
Pero nagiging lusot nga eh.

**[01:42:52.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001360)  
Itong mga ganitong butas para mapanatili na perpetually contractual yung karamihan ng mga manggagawa na po pwede naman irregular.

**[01:43:03.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001361)  
Alam niyo po, last year during the plenary budget deliberation, yung CSC sinabi, kahit ngayon po sinasabi niyo, labas sa jurisdiction niyo ang mga JO at COS.

**[01:43:14.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001362)  
At yan ay nasa regulasyon ng DBM.

**[01:43:17.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001363)  
Pero tinanong din po natin ang DBM.

**[01:43:19.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001364)  
Ang tinuro, pabalik kayo, CSC.

**[01:43:22.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001365)  
Sabi, kay Rojo ang may problema.

**[01:43:23.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001366)  
Yung qualification standards niyo, eh kailangan nang i-review.

**[01:43:27.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001367)  
So ano po ang comment dito ng Civil Service Commission?

**[01:43:29.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001368)  
Kasi nagtuturuan po tayo dito eh.

**[01:43:31.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001369)  
Tuturo ng CSC, DBM.

**[01:43:33.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001370)  
Sasabi ng DBM, wala, wala sa aming problema.

**[01:43:35.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001371)  
Problema nandun sa CSC.

**[01:43:37.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-001372)  
Madam Chairperson.

**[01:43:39.040] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001373)  
Kung wala, hindi ko.

**[01:43:41.920] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001374)  
Ayoko nung nagpapasa-pasa ng ano yung turo.

**[01:43:44.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001375)  
In charge po sa qualification standards kami.

**[01:43:49.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001376)  
Pero pag-create ng positions, hindi na.

**[01:43:51.960] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001377)  
Tinanggal sa Civil Service yan eh.

**[01:43:54.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001378)  
Classification ng positions, tinanggal sa amin yan eh.

**[01:43:57.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001379)  
Pero sa mandato namin na talaga, sa Constitution of EO 292,

**[01:44:02.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001380)  
yan dapat ay magkakasama.

**[01:44:03.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001381)  
Kasi integrated na system yan eh.

**[01:44:05.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001382)  
Yung count mo ng manpower, yung deployment ng manpower,

**[01:44:09.220] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001383)  
qualification standards, position qualification,

**[01:44:11.620] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001384)  
dapat iisang integrated na sistema.

**[01:44:14.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001385)  
Pinaghiwalay yung sistema.

**[01:44:16.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001386)  
This is what we are living on right now.

**[01:44:19.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001387)  
Ngayon, hindi ko talaga i-deny, hindi ito cop out na hindi namin sila saklaw.

**[01:44:25.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001388)  
Kasi talagang sa mandato namin hindi kasama ang JOs and COs

**[01:44:29.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001389)  
by the very definition of what they essentially are.

**[01:44:32.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001390)  
But whether or not they are important, they are.

**[01:44:35.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001391)  
Kasi kung hindi, abutin tayo ng siyam-siyam sa pag-appoint

**[01:44:38.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001392)  
ng nangangailangan na emergency employment or necessary additional employment

**[01:44:43.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001393)  
para sa atin.

**[01:44:44.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001394)  
Atin mga critical government agencies.

**[01:44:47.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001395)  
So I think there is much sense, there is much logic doon sa pre-recent ni Ryan,

**[01:44:54.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001396)  
which has been put out time and time again sa Commission.

**[01:44:58.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001397)  
But if you would read closely.

**[01:45:00.000] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001398)  
the provisions of the Joint Memo Circular

**[01:45:02.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001399)  
ng DBM-COA

**[01:45:04.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001400)  
and CSC.

**[01:45:07.060] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001401)  
Nakalagay doon

**[01:45:07.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001402)  
ang responsibilidad ng mga ahensya

**[01:45:10.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001403)  
that they should maintain an

**[01:45:12.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001404)  
updated inventory of their JOs

**[01:45:14.160] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001405)  
and COs where they are deployed

**[01:45:16.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001406)  
kasama sa optimization yan eh.

**[01:45:18.360] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001407)  
Where they are deployed in the agency,

**[01:45:20.580] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001408)  
what kind of functions

**[01:45:22.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001409)  
they are doing,

**[01:45:24.260] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001410)  
and let's do some analytics on them.

**[01:45:26.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001411)  
Doon natin i-address.

**[01:45:27.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001412)  
At i-decentralize natin ang pag-address.

**[01:45:30.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001413)  
Yung agency, kasi

**[01:45:31.540] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001414)  
optimization nga yung punto,

**[01:45:33.320] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001415)  
yung agency, kasi siyang maghahanap

**[01:45:35.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001416)  
ng tamang istruktura at pulisiya

**[01:45:37.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001417)  
are relevant sa kanyang particular

**[01:45:39.840] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001418)  
na mandato. So kung magawa

**[01:45:41.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001419)  
nila ito sa bawat ahensya,

**[01:45:43.880] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001420)  
which is a mandate of the Joint Circular,

**[01:45:46.700] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001421)  
DBM, lahat

**[01:45:47.940] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001422)  
ng national agencies,

**[01:45:49.900] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001423)  
the LGUs are under the supervision

**[01:45:51.680] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001424)  
of the Executive Department.

**[01:45:53.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001425)  
So if this can be strictly

**[01:45:55.660] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001426)  
implemented, the data that

**[01:45:57.600] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001427)  
we need to create

**[01:45:59.760] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001428)  
craft policies appropriate

**[01:46:01.720] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001429)  
to

**[01:46:02.340] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001430)  
enable

**[01:46:04.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001431)  
more effectively our JOs

**[01:46:07.640] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001432)  
and COs to get into government

**[01:46:09.500] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001433)  
service, ay mas may

**[01:46:11.280] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001434)  
evidence-based ba? As of now,

**[01:48:12.080] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001492)

**[01:48:13.460] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001493)

**[01:48:15.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001494)

**[01:48:21.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001497)

**[01:48:33.200] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001504)

**[01:48:35.020] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001505)

**[01:48:41.420] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-001508)

eh on the part of the CSC, we interpret 11.2 of the JMC provision as giving the benefit to the JOCOS to be entitled to the Solo Parent Leave Act. Pero CSC lang po ito. Bahala si DBM at COA kung anong positioning nila on the matter.

**[02:00:41.800] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001832)  
But with respect to CSC, nag-usap kami, ganun yung interpretation po namin na covered ang JOCOS doon sa provision of law granting solo parent leave sa mga tatay yata yung nakalagay doon na 7 days.

**[02:00:57.780] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001833)

**[02:01:11.240] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001834)  
Ang mga JOCOS to air out their grievances kung anong may mga reklamo sila. But the nature of the contract, Madam Chair, is that yun yung principally nag-govern sa relationship ng JOCOS at ng agency na pinaglilingkuran nila.

**[02:01:29.340] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-001835)

Madam Chair, the contact center ng bayan in the Civil Service Commission is a program where the citizens can directly report any violation or any anomalies that they observe from different agencies of the government.

**[02:05:44.890] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-001877)

So, sa madalang salita, you are not

**[02:29:30.730] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002384)  
encouraging, ba?

**[02:29:32.630] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002385)  
It's not encouraging. Ang hirap talaga

**[02:29:34.830] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002386)  
ba talagang CSC?

**[02:29:36.870] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002387)  
The reason po,

**[02:29:38.870] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002388)  
Madam Chair, that I take the examination

**[02:29:41.010] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002389)  
every time

**[02:29:42.930] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002390)  
we conduct the examination myself.

**[02:29:44.710] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002391)  
Bago po kami mag-conduct, tinitake ko yung examination.

**[02:29:47.150] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002392)  
And I can

**[02:29:48.490] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002393)  
vouch before you, na fair

**[02:29:50.950] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002394)  
po yung mga items sa examinations

**[02:29:52.710] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002395)  
namin. I think the low passing

**[02:29:54.930] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002396)  
rate in our examination is an indictment

**[02:29:56.790] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002397)  
of our standard of education

**[02:29:58.570] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002398)  
in the Philippines and not the indictment

**[02:30:00.000] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002399)  
of the quality of the questions we have

**[02:30:02.220] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002400)  
in our civil service examination.

**[02:30:04.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002401)  
Hindi po sa pangmamayabang, pero galing po akong

**[02:30:06.160] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002402)  
Jakarta, Puyat ako, magte-take ako

**[02:30:08.100] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002403)  
ng exam, pero I'm getting 90 plus.

**[02:30:10.820] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002404)  
So, hindi po siya sa quality ng

**[02:30:12.140] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002405)  
questions, but I think it's

**[02:30:14.160] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002406)  
the quality of the people taking the examinations.

**[02:30:16.700] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002407)  
So, I'm sorry to say po

**[02:30:18.400] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002408)  
na medyo talaga mababa po

**[02:30:19.980] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002409)  
yung standard. At hindi po

**[02:30:22.120] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002410)  
siya comparable sa mga professional examination

**[02:30:24.420] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002411)  
na homogenous po yung nagte-take.

**[02:30:26.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002412)  
They studied particularly for that

**[02:30:28.160] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002413)  
particular examination. Sa civil

**[02:30:30.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002414)  
service exams po, aptitude siya.

**[02:30:32.300] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002415)  
So, it cuts. Sobrang

**[02:30:34.320] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002416)  
heterogeneous po yung nag-a-apply doon.

**[02:30:36.500] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002417)  
Pwedeng antagal na, pwedeng

**[02:30:37.900] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002418)  
estudyante pa lang. So, hindi po siya

**[02:30:40.220] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002419)  
mako-compare sa passing rate. And again po,

**[02:30:42.520] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002420)  
if we go to the bottom line of the problem,

**[02:30:44.340] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002421)  
our problem is not the number of eligibles.

**[02:30:46.760] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002422)  
Kaya hindi po problema yung

**[02:30:48.140] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002423)  
low passing rate ng civil

**[02:30:50.100] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002424)  
service eligibility because we're

**[02:30:52.160] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002425)  
producing

**[02:30:55.880] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002426)  
150

**[02:30:56.480] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002427)  
or

**[02:30:57.040] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002428)  
145,000

**[02:30:58.460] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002429)  
surplus per year

**[02:30:59.880] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002430)  
ng mga eligibles po.

**[02:31:01.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002431)  
So, Mr. Commissioner, you're now saying

**[02:31:04.220] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002432)  
na low quality ang mga examiners natin

**[02:31:06.000] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002433)  
sa Pilipinas?

**[02:31:06.860] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002433-split-2)  
Yung standard po

**[02:31:08.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002434)  
ng competency natin mukhang mababa.

**[02:31:10.260] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002435)  
I think, hindi lang po ako nagsasabi

**[02:31:12.180] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002436)  
may mga... That's okay. I just need

**[02:31:14.140] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002437)  
to get from you. So, Commissioner,

**[02:31:16.460] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002438)  
that's an official statement from CSC.

**[02:31:17.900] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002439)  
No po. Personal po.

**[02:31:18.920] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002439-split-2)  
Na low quality, or your personal

**[02:31:19.940] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002440)  
opinion, na low quality talaga

**[02:31:22.160] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002441)  
ang mga examiners. That's why hindi pumapasa

**[02:31:24.040] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002442)  
The current standard po

**[02:31:25.960] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002443)  
ng comprehension and ano ng mga examiners

**[02:31:28.160] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002444)  
natin, I think, as verified

**[02:31:30.120] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002445)  
by the index ng education

**[02:31:32.300] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002446)  
natin, na nag-viral

**[02:31:34.380] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002447)  
recently, medyo mababa po.

**[02:31:35.580] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002448)  
I'm limited sa 5 minutes kasi. Sorry lang ha.

**[02:31:37.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002449)  
So, will you support yung equivalency

**[02:31:40.220] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002450)  
bill ko?

**[02:31:41.740] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002451)  
If you've been working 15 years,

**[02:31:44.560] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002452)  
10 years, in fact,

**[02:31:46.200] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002453)  
when I was a subcommittee chair,

**[02:31:48.780] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002454)  
other than saying 15 years,

**[02:31:50.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002455)  
we said at least 3 years

**[02:31:52.160] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002456)  
of good service, na

**[02:31:54.180] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002457)  
maganda yung record mo,

**[02:31:56.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002458)  
you're now eligible

**[02:31:57.560] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002459)  
to be. Para matapos

**[02:32:00.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002460)  
yung unfilled positions natin.

**[02:32:02.080] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002461)  
Ang dami kasi. Hindi po

**[02:32:03.920] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002462)  
kasi siya panagot sa unfilled position

**[02:32:05.860] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002463)  
kasi sobrang-sobrang nga po ng

**[02:32:07.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002464)  
eligibles tayo, pero hindi pa rin siya na-fulfill upon.

**[02:32:10.420] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002465)  
So, ang problema rin po

**[02:32:12.000] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002466)  
naman kasi pag, porke

**[02:32:13.580] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002467)  
nag-JOS ka at COS ka,

**[02:32:15.740] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002468)  
pag nag-3 years ka,

**[02:32:17.460] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002469)  
equivalent ka na, it will encourage

**[02:32:19.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002470)  
LGUs to just hire people na hindi

**[02:32:21.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002471)  
qualified, and then pag na-reach nila yung

**[02:32:23.580] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002472)  
3 years, qualified na sila. So, we're

**[02:32:26.060] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002473)  
actually perpetuating the

**[02:32:27.560] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002474)  
system where we hire

**[02:32:29.280] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002475)  
unqualified people, and porke

**[02:32:31.660] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002476)  
tumagal sila sa posisyon, magiging

**[02:32:33.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002477)  
ano na sila, magiging qualified nila sila.

**[02:32:35.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002478)  
Bottom line po kasi, mandato ng CSC,

**[02:32:38.040] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002479)  
we have to ensure merit and

**[02:32:39.600] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002480)  
fitness in government. Kaya po, hirap na

**[02:32:41.560] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002481)  
hirap kaming medyo, yung

**[02:32:43.700] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002482)  
halfway na nga po yung pagbigay namin

**[02:32:45.480] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002483)  
ng preference rating na

**[02:32:46.920] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002484)  
pag nagtagal ka sa gobyerno,

**[02:32:50.000] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002485)  
tapos nag-exam ka

**[02:32:51.860] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002486)  
at kinulang ka ng konti, may plus ka.

**[02:32:54.080] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002487)  
Pero, on my part, personal ko po,

**[02:32:55.900] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002488)  
I don't know with the commission, kasi

**[02:32:57.560] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002489)  
isa lang naman po ako sa tatlo, kung

**[02:32:59.460] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002490)  
magpo-fly po yung equivalency.

**[02:33:01.940] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002491)  
Pero, personal opinion ko po,

**[02:33:03.940] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002492)  
mukhang maa-undermine yung merit

**[02:33:05.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002493)  
and fitness kung 3 years,

**[02:33:07.600] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002494)  
eh, automatic na, eh, eligible

**[02:33:09.540] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002495)  
na po siya. Pero, pag-aaralan po namin yan,

**[02:33:11.400] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002496)  
pagdi-diskusyonan namin sa commission, pag-aaway-awayan

**[02:33:13.940] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002497)  
namin, if need be, para po

**[02:33:15.480] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002498)  
magkaroon ng reasonable

**[02:33:16.740] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002499)  
resolution po yung concern nyo,

**[02:33:19.460] CSC Commissioner Ryan Alvin R. Acosta** (asr-002500)  
Madon Chair.

**[02:33:38.910] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002501)  
Halfway to permanent for

**[02:33:40.910] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002502)  
qualified government workers to render

**[02:33:42.770] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002503)  
at least 3 years. Yun lang.

**[02:33:45.030] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002504)  
And I hope that you can support

**[02:33:47.110] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002505)  
that in order,

**[02:33:48.810] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002506)  
as I said,

**[02:33:50.830] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002507)  
you seem to be very passionate,

**[02:33:53.350] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002508)  
but,

**[02:33:54.350] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002509)  
I don't see a light in the tunnel.

**[02:33:57.770] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002510)  
How are we going to solve this?

**[02:33:59.530] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002511)  
So, that's

**[02:34:00.590] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002512)  
House Bill 93 for you.

**[02:34:03.850] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002513)  
On another topic lang.

**[02:34:06.190] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002514)  
Last point lang, last point.

**[02:34:10.980] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002515)  
Pwede ba?

**[02:34:11.660] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002516)  
Mendoza, yes.

**[02:34:12.740] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002517)  
House Bill 92 proposes

**[02:34:15.980] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002518)  
an independent PSL,

**[02:34:17.780] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002519)  
LRB.

**[02:34:19.800] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002520)  
92, I'm sorry.

**[02:34:21.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002521)  
Which is actually, because we ratify

**[02:34:23.480] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002522)  
ILO Convention 151.

**[02:34:26.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002523)  
Now,

**[02:34:28.260] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002524)  
to bring about impartiality sa labor

**[02:34:30.020] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002525)  
dispute, parang NRC.

**[02:34:32.400] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002526)  
The problem there, we have to create

**[02:34:34.220] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002527)  
the PS,

**[02:34:35.480] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002528)  
PS,

**[02:34:37.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002529)  
Public Relations,

**[02:34:39.100] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002530)  
Public Relations

**[02:34:41.380] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002531)  
Board.

**[02:34:43.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002532)  
Kasi PSLMC, ngayon, di ba?

**[02:34:49.740] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002533)  
Exactly.

**[02:34:51.360] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002534)  
What we're trying to say, because we ratified

**[02:34:53.720] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002535)  
it in 2017, ma'am,

**[02:34:55.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002536)  
we're trying to say is that

**[02:34:57.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002537)  
ang CSC is the personal manager,

**[02:35:00.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002538)  
di ba? And then, usually,

**[02:35:01.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002539)  
ang reklamo papunta dyan.

**[02:35:03.480] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002540)  
But you also decide on it.

**[02:35:05.800] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002541)  
Something like NLRC,

**[02:35:08.020] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002542)  
na tripartite, ba?

**[02:35:09.100] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002543)  
That there are representatives

**[02:35:11.000] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002544)  
from government. In the present

**[02:35:13.460] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002545)  
set of the E0180,

**[02:35:16.900] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002546)  
parang walang

**[02:35:17.460] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002547)  
labor representative. Are you open

**[02:35:19.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002548)  
to that, Madam Chair?

**[02:35:23.140] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002549)  
There are labor representatives

**[02:35:25.120] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002550)  
po sa PSLMC.

**[02:35:27.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002551)  
In fact, nagkukondak sila ng

**[02:35:29.180] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002552)  
regional congresses right now.

**[02:35:31.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002553)  
And then, from the congresses, they will

**[02:35:33.400] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002554)  
nominate labor sector representatives.

**[02:35:35.580] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002555)  
Exactly, Madam Chair, that I would like

**[02:35:37.300] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002556)  
to point out. I hope

**[02:35:39.500] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002557)  
and I pray na

**[02:35:41.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002558)  
observers sila ngayon. Observers lang.

**[02:35:44.180] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002559)  
They should be a full-fledged

**[02:35:45.600] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002560)  
member or able to vote.

**[02:35:47.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002561)  
Members lang ngayon, eh. Yes, they do.

**[02:35:49.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002562)  
Pero members, ano lang, observer.

**[02:35:51.540] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002563)  
Parang ano lang sa gilid-gilid, ba?

**[02:35:53.020] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002564)  
They just observe. So,

**[02:35:55.360] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002565)  
this one naman, they already passed in the committee level.

**[02:35:58.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002566)  
And I hope also that you will

**[02:35:59.620] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002567)  
also support this one.

**[02:36:01.860] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002568)  
This is very important in terms

**[02:36:03.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002569)  
of labor management

**[02:36:05.040] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002570)  
of government workers.

**[02:36:07.680] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002571)  
Medyo kapusta kong panahon, Madam Chair.

**[02:36:10.700] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002572)  
Importante lang.

**[02:36:11.640] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002573)  
Because we also are the signatory of

**[02:36:13.560] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002574)  
an ILO convention. I should know. I was the one

**[02:36:15.600] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002575)  
who pressed for that and it was already

**[02:36:17.520] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002576)  
ratified.

**[02:36:19.660] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002577)  
By the Senate and signed by the President,

**[02:37:50.950] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002630)  
Alright.

**[02:37:51.770] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002631)  
Brian Poe, you are recognized.

**[02:37:53.570] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002632)  
Sige po.

**[02:37:54.370] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002633)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:37:55.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002634)  
Thank you, of course, to the Civil Service Commission for making the time to be here today.

**[02:38:02.210] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002635)  
Yun nga po.

**[02:38:03.130] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002636)  
I just want to follow up on my questions from last year.

**[02:38:06.370] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002637)  
So last year, I brought up the concern regarding cyber security.

**[02:38:09.910] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002638)  
Yung hacking na nangyari nung August.

**[02:38:12.790] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002639)  
Now, I want to understand where we are in terms of cyber security for the Civil Service Commission.

**[02:38:18.850] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002640)  
I went through your budget proposal for NEP.

**[02:38:21.630] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002641)  
Parang wala po ako nakitang specific item for...

**[02:38:25.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002642)  
for cyber security.

**[02:38:27.610] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002643)  
Pero meron po kayong 1.4 billion pesos para sa modernization.

**[02:38:32.750] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002644)  
Meron po ba tayong subcategory dito para sa cyber?

**[02:38:37.910] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002645)  
Integral po doon sa Prime EGS at saka sa HRMISPS yung cyber security.

**[02:38:45.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002646)  
Pero aside from the provisions in the PCSMP, we are now drafting a manual.

**[02:38:54.630] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002647)  
Parang...

**[02:38:55.510] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002648)  
It will be circularized, but it is a data privacy and cyber security manual.

**[02:39:01.530] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002649)  
It's taking us a little bit of time kasi pinagdudugtong namin yung data privacy

**[02:39:06.330] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002650)  
at saka yung latest na cyber security issuances from the House

**[02:39:10.350] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002651)  
and yung implementation schema ng DICT.

**[02:39:16.310] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002652)  
Kasama po, integral na po sa lahat ng aming digital programs for the next three years

**[02:39:21.650] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002653)  
ay robust cyber security provisions.

**[02:39:25.510] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002654)  
Kami po ang isa sa mga pinaka-porous na organizations dito sa ano.

**[02:39:31.710] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002655)  
Lalo na at ano ba yun?

**[02:39:33.970] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002656)  
Analog lahat karamihan ng aming datos.

**[02:39:36.630] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002657)  
So ngayon ay medyo ano na kami.

**[02:39:39.110] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002658)  
Mas watchful na kami dito sa data privacy plus cyber security.

**[02:39:44.790] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002659)  
Wala po magandang digitalization kung hindi po integral ang cyber security.

**[02:39:50.910] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002660)  
Tama po yan, Madam Chair.

**[02:39:52.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002661)  
Madam Chair, gusto ko malaman kung magkano talaga...

**[02:39:55.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002662)  
yung naka-allocate para sa cyber security.

**[02:39:58.670] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002663)  
Meron po tayong hard figure?

**[02:40:00.470] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002664)  
Meron na ba?

**[02:40:01.890] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002665)  
Kasi kailangan po bantayan yan sa budget.

**[02:40:03.930] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002666)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:40:04.590] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002667)  
We cannot fix a specific ano kasi naka-integrate siya ngayon dun sa programa

**[02:40:08.570] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002668)  
but can we submit it to you after this meeting

**[02:40:11.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002669)  
para ma-disaggregate niya yung para sa Prime ESG, para sa HRMISPS

**[02:40:17.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002670)  
at saka yung para sa dun sa other digital programs ng CSC.

**[02:40:23.150] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002671)  
Yes, po.

**[02:40:24.090] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002672)  
Madam Chair, I'll request that...

**[02:40:25.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002673)  
I'll request that from the Civil Service Commission kasi ang ayoko mangyari baka mamaya

**[02:40:30.890] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002674)  
yung budget para sa cyber security mabawasan or matanggal dahil hindi namin napansin

**[02:40:37.130] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002675)  
or lumped in siya sa ibang budget item.

**[02:40:41.170] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002676)  
Critical po yung cyber security natin para sa civil service

**[02:40:44.450] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002677)  
because as I see here, you're now going very hard into digital services, no?

**[02:40:50.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002678)  
In fact, you have new rules regarding your salen

**[02:40:54.630] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002679)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:40:55.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002680)  
to now be submitted via online oath-taking, online filing, and digital submission.

**[02:41:00.970] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002681)  
Tama po ba?

**[02:41:01.510] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002682)  
Yes, po.

**[02:41:01.930] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002683)  
Yes, po.

**[02:41:02.650] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002684)  
So, I want to ask about this online filing ng salen.

**[02:41:06.890] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002685)  
Ano yung mga safety measures or authentication measures that you're putting in place?

**[02:41:13.830] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002686)  
Kasi syempre, online oath-taking, et cetera, paano natin ma-verify na totoong oath-taking yung nangyari

**[02:41:20.290] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002687)  
at hindi magiging lusot yan later on na hindi po ako yan?

**[02:41:25.010] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002688)  
May I ask as Com. Kilio?

**[02:41:32.090] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002689)  
In-charge po siya doon sa oath-taking and other digital signatures for our program.

**[02:41:36.710] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002690)  
Opo. Thank you po.

**[02:41:37.730] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002691)  
Madam Chair, sir, doon po sa ating rules, the new omnibus rules on salen,

**[02:41:44.250] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002692)  
yung pong online oath-taking, meron pong very specific procedure on how it will be taken.

**[02:41:50.690] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002693)  
At ang first step po doon ay yung verification ng identification.

**[02:41:55.010] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002694)  
It is ng oath administrator at saka yung person who is taking the oath.

**[02:42:00.030] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002695)  
So, unless that is duly verified, hindi po magpo-proceed yung oath-taking.

**[02:42:05.150] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002696)  
So, if the oath administrator is convinced that the one taking the oath is really the person na may-ari ng salen,

**[02:42:14.030] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002697)  
that's the only time that he will allow it.

**[02:42:16.950] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002698)  
I want to understand, ano yung authentication process nyo?

**[02:42:20.610] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002699)  
Is it just mag-zoom tayo tapos i-coconvert?

**[02:42:25.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002700)  
Affirm na ako yung kausap mo doon sa Zoom? Ganun po ba?

**[02:42:28.930] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002701)  
Opo. Mag-zoom po and then the oath administrator will ask questions about the identity of the one taking the oath.

**[02:42:38.350] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002702)  
So that he will be personally convinced that the one taking the oath is really the owner of the salen.

**[02:42:45.310] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002703)  
I'm all for efficiency, no?

**[02:42:47.290] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002704)  
Pero nakakatakot na talaga yung AI technology.

**[02:42:49.950] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002705)  
May mga AI technology na pwede mong gamitin para mag-iiba tayo.

**[02:42:55.010] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002706)  
Kaya talaga yung itsura mo to look like someone else completely in a live feed between people.

**[02:43:01.750] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002707)  
Ang daming naloloko dito sa mga scams, etc.

**[02:43:04.590] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002708)  
Ang takot ko, baka mamaya sa oath-taking for something as important as the salen,

**[02:43:09.410] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002709)  
baka may mag-impersonate pa ng tao dito.

**[02:43:12.710] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002710)  
Ede, yari yung public official or yung government official kung mali yung salen tapos AI pala yun.

**[02:43:20.810] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002711)  
Ano yung authentication process natin?

**[02:43:22.590] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002712)  
Hindi pwede, parang zoom lang.

**[02:43:28.120] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002713)  
Nakalagay naman po doon, all precaution should be taken to ensure the real identities of the persons involved.

**[02:43:42.660] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002714)  
So, thank you po for that advice.

**[02:43:46.260] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002715)  
I think all oath administrators ought to do that.

**[02:43:50.100] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002716)  
Kasi kung nagkataon na iba yung nag-undertake ng oath sa kanya, baka pa rin siya magkaroon ng liability.

**[02:43:56.240] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002717)  
And I think everyone who is given...

**[02:43:58.120] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002718)  
And the authority to administer oath is given due notice that they have to take all the necessary precautions

**[02:44:04.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002719)  
to ensure that the online oath-taking is really genuine and is not involving a fictitious oath-taker, Madam Chair.

**[02:44:13.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002720)  
Madam Chair, may I ask if they're partnering with DICT or PNP or NBI,

**[02:44:19.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002721)  
any law enforcement to guarantee the authenticity of these online oath-takings?

**[02:44:24.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002722)  
Okay.

**[02:44:26.880] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002723)  
DICT, do we have...

**[02:44:28.120] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002724)  
We have a continuing partnership po with the DICT with respect to all ICT matters, Mr. Chair, Madam Chair.

**[02:44:41.400] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002725)  
Thank you po for that advice.

**[02:44:42.960] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002726)  
I think we will include this in the concerns that we can raise with them.

**[02:44:46.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002727)  
It's one of those things kasi salin is a very sacred thing to all of us public officials.

**[02:44:51.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002728)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[02:44:52.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002729)  
And we want to make sure that we protect both those filing and those who are administering,

**[02:44:58.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002730)  
the oath and the data protection

**[02:45:00.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002731)  
yes madam chair

**[02:45:03.290] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002731-split-2)  
okay so with that being said i noticed here you were talking about

**[02:45:06.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002732)  
developing your SALN. Do we have specific guidelines on the use of the provision

**[02:45:13.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002733)  
under others? Kung ano yung puwedeng ilagay doon sa others category sa loob ng SALN?

**[02:45:14.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002734)  
Because I don't want to assume that anyone would misfile their SALN and try to

**[02:45:25.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002735)  
tuck things under others. But others isn't necessarily broken down. May guidelines po ba kayo

**[02:45:29.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002736)  
kung ano yung puwede nating ilagay doon sa others or i-categorize as others?

**[02:45:34.640] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002737)  
Madam Chair, may I? Madam Chair, yes, I think, Madam Chair, you are referring to that portion of the SALN,

**[02:45:44.720] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002738)  
referring to personal properties. Because in the Declaration of Personal Properties,

**[02:45:51.280] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002739)  
it can be lumped together or it can be

**[02:45:54.780] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002740)  
joined together,

**[02:45:55.240] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002741)  
and the value can be estimated. That's why it is lumped as others.

**[02:46:02.360] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002742)  
Dati po, talagang it is very generally stated. You can jointly declare all your personal properties even

**[02:46:12.060] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002743)  
without specifying them.

**[02:46:13.500] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002744)  
Pero sa 2025 SALN po,

**[02:46:15.060] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002745)  
clarify natin.

**[02:46:16.200] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002746)  
Pagka po

**[02:46:18.500] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002747)  
cash

**[02:46:20.560] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002748)  
cash

**[02:46:22.680] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002749)  
in bank or cash on hand,

**[02:46:24.840] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002750)  
dapat

**[02:46:25.220] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002751)  
separately declared po.

**[02:46:26.720] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002752)  
At yung mga

**[02:46:28.020] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002753)  
personal properties po na registered, such as motor vehicles, guns, among others,

**[02:46:37.060] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002754)  
kapag po iyan nire-register sa kahit anong government agencies,

**[02:46:42.160] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002755)  
hindi ho puwedeng i-lump lang iyan sa others. It has to be individually registered. And we made that very clear

**[02:46:45.140] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002756)  
in the new rules on SALN.

**[02:46:46.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002757)  
madam chair

**[02:46:49.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002758)  
madam chair

**[02:46:49.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002759)  
can you fight congressman

**[02:46:50.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002760)  
I do not want to make a mistake

**[02:46:52.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002761)  
so if you have the right guidelines

**[02:46:54.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002762)  
and susundan po natin yan.

**[02:46:57.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002763)  
Especially meron na tayong

**[02:46:58.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002764)  
online electronic filing.

**[02:47:00.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002765)  
Ayaw ko magkamali dito sa online

**[02:47:02.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002766)  
filing po natin.

**[02:47:04.320] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002767)  
Nadun po yung finlash namin kanina.

**[02:47:06.280] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002768)  
One of the new major issuances

**[02:47:08.560] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002769)  
of the Civil Service Commission po in

**[02:47:10.560] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002770)  
2025. Thank you po, Madam Chair.

**[02:48:04.900] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002797)  
detailed

**[02:48:07.140] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002798)

**[02:48:08.980] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002799)

**[02:48:10.580] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002800)

**[02:48:12.280] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002801)

**[02:48:15.240] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002802)

**[02:48:19.420] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002804)

**[02:48:20.980] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002805)

**[02:48:23.440] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002806)  
that they will,

**[02:48:24.600] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002807)

**[02:48:29.040] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002809)

**[02:48:31.080] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002810)

**[02:48:33.060] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002811)

**[02:48:34.800] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002812)

Na dapat meron silang

**[02:48:38.420] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002814)

**[02:48:40.820] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002815)

**[02:48:43.240] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002816)  
So apparently, yung pong

**[02:48:45.080] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002817)

**[02:48:47.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002818)

**[02:48:48.800] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002819)

**[02:48:51.020] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002820)

**[02:48:53.840] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002821)  
That's why our

**[02:48:55.760] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002823)

So this is exactly the type of

**[02:49:20.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002835)

**[02:49:22.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002836)  
Not just the use of budget,

**[02:49:24.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002837)  
but protecting our constituents,

**[02:49:26.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002838)

with that, I hope that they

**[02:49:34.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002842)  
of reference to protect our

**[02:49:36.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002843)  
privacy. And

**[02:49:38.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002844)  
so, Madam Chair, regarding

**[02:49:40.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002845)  
this budget, I hope it's not taken

**[02:49:42.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002846)  
against the Civil Service that

**[02:49:44.220] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002847)  
they had to declare

**[02:49:46.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002848)
