**[02:49:48.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002849)  
because of the violations of our

**[02:49:50.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002850)  
data privacy.

**[02:49:52.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002851)  
Yan lang, Madam Chair.

**[02:49:54.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002852)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:49:55.520] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002853)  
Thank you so much.

**[02:49:59.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002854)  
Vice Chair

**[02:50:00.500] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002855)  
Brian Paul, next to

**[02:50:02.560] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002856)  
interpellate is Representative

**[02:50:04.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002857)  
Isabel Sagabaria.

**[02:50:07.980] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002858)  
Thank you,

**[02:50:08.900] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002859)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:50:12.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002860)  
Mayong hapon sa tanan.

**[02:50:16.760] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002861)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Mayong hapon sa tanan.

**[02:50:29.800] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002863)  
Madam Chair,

**[02:50:31.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002864)  
I would like to raise a question

**[02:50:33.160] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002865)  
regarding the scope of

**[02:50:35.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002866)  
Civil Service Commission's authority

**[02:50:37.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002867)  
in defining and

**[02:50:39.380] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002868)  
regulating nepotism.

**[02:50:42.160] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002869)  
Section 59,

**[02:50:43.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002870)  
Book 5 of Executive Order

**[02:50:45.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002871)  
No. 292, or

**[02:50:47.260] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002872)  
the Administrative Code of

**[02:50:48.740] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002873)  
1987, expressly

**[02:50:51.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002874)  
defines the relatives covered by

**[02:50:53.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002875)  
the prohibition on nepotism

**[02:50:55.080] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002876)  
as those within the third degree

**[02:50:57.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002877)  
of consanguinity or affinity.

**[02:50:59.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002878)  
On the other hand,

**[02:51:01.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002879)  
Section 79 of Republic

**[02:51:03.400] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002880)  
Act No. 7160,

**[02:51:05.620] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002881)  
or the Local Government Code,

**[02:51:07.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002882)  
provides for a fourth-degree

**[02:51:08.820] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002883)  
prohibition in the case of nepotistic

**[02:51:11.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002884)  
appointments by appointing

**[02:51:13.480] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002885)  
authorities.

**[02:51:15.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002886)  
Likewise, Section 3

**[02:51:17.100] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002887)  
of Republic Act No.

**[02:51:19.320] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002888)  
6713, or the

**[02:51:21.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002889)  
Code of Conduct and Ethical Standards

**[02:51:23.160] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002890)  
for Public Officials and Employees,

**[02:51:25.640] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002891)  
expressly refers to

**[02:51:27.340] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002892)  
relatives within the fourth

**[02:51:29.160] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002893)  
civil degree of consanguinity,

**[02:51:31.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002894)  
or the Code of Conduct and Ethical Standards for Public Officials and Employees,

**[02:51:31.320] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002895)  
or Workshop or Affinity.

**[02:51:32.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002896)  
These are statutory provisions

**[02:51:35.060] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002897)  
that specifically identify

**[02:51:37.480] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002898)  
both the degree and

**[02:51:39.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002899)  
nature of the relationship covered

**[02:51:41.320] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002900)  
by the prohibition.

**[02:51:43.800] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002901)  
However, the

**[02:51:45.000] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002902)  
2025 ORA-ORA,

**[02:51:47.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002903)  
or Omnibus Rules on Appointments

**[02:51:49.420] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002904)  
and Other Human Resources

**[02:51:50.740] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002905)  
Action, provides that

**[02:51:53.360] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002906)  
for purposes of the

**[02:51:55.120] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002907)  
rule on nepotism,

**[02:51:57.180] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002908)  
a common law relationship

**[02:51:58.780] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002909)  
shall be considered equivalent to

**[02:52:01.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002910)  
marital relationship. My question is, what is the specific statutory basis for the CSC

**[02:52:09.240] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002911)  
to make this additional classification? More importantly, Madam Chair, does this not raise

**[02:52:16.440] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002912)  
a concern under the fundamental principle that an administrative agency cannot rise

**[02:52:22.480] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002913)  
above its enabling law, that the spring cannot rise above its source? If Congress has itself

**[02:52:29.620] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002914)  
specifically defined the relationships and degrees covered by the statutory prohibition,

**[02:52:36.160] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002915)  
would the CSC's inclusion of common law relationships effectively expand, modify,

**[02:52:42.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002916)  
or add to a substantive prohibition that Congress itself has prescribed? I fully recognize the

**[02:52:49.860] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002917)  
authority of the CSC to promulgate rules and necessary to implement civil service laws.

**[02:52:57.660] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002918)  
But where the implementing rule

**[02:52:59.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002919)  
creates an additional category of persons subject to a legal prohibition, where do we draw the line

**[02:53:06.920] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002920)  
between implementation and legislation? Would the Commission agree that while an administrative

**[02:53:14.020] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002921)  
agency may fill in the details necessary to carry out a law, it cannot enlarge the scope

**[02:53:21.220] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002922)  
of a statutory prohibition or create a new substantive restriction absent clear authority

**[02:53:28.620] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002923)  
from Congress?

**[02:53:29.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002924)  
And finally, if the CSC believes that common law relationship should be covered by the prohibition

**[02:53:37.320] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002925)  
on nepotism, should that policy not properly be addressed through legislation rather than

**[02:53:44.300] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002926)  
through an administrative rule? I raise this question not to question the objective of

**[02:53:53.460] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002927)  
preventing nepotism, but to clarify the limits of administrative rulemaking and to

**[02:53:59.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002928)  
ensure that the Commission's regulations remain firmly anchored on the law and acted by Congress.

**[02:54:06.280] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002929)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:54:08.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-002930)  
As Comrade Romeo will answer the question, please.

**[02:54:12.940] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002931)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. First, we want to recognize the valid position of the Honorable

**[02:54:20.900] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002932)  
Congresswoman, and we agree that, of course, if there is no legal basis, we cannot,

**[02:54:27.660] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002933)  
by administrative,

**[02:54:29.600] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002934)  
fiat, come up with something that will be later found to be prejudicial to persons involved.

**[02:54:38.760] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002935)  
But I will beg the indulgence of the honourable members of the committee to also understand

**[02:54:45.280] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002936)  
where the commission is coming from.

**[02:54:47.520] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002937)  
Nepotism is an offence defined under our laws and the purpose of prohibiting nepotism is

**[02:54:58.480] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002938)  
to ensure that appointments in the civil service should only be made according to merit and

**[02:55:03.400] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002939)  
fitness and that any form of favoritism is not used as basis for the issuance of an appointment

**[02:55:12.540] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002940)  
to any position in government.

**[02:55:15.800] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002941)  
Nepotism is a prohibition against appointing relatives.

**[02:55:20.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002942)  
That is correct.

**[02:55:20.880] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002943)  
But the underlying consideration is that if a relative is appointed to a position that

**[02:55:27.100] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002944)  
requires merit and fitness,

**[02:55:28.480] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002945)  
the consideration is not actually merit and fitness but the relationship itself.

**[02:55:34.840] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002946)  
And according to a number of jurisprudence, what cannot be done directly cannot be done indirectly.

**[02:55:43.260] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002947)  
If you cannot extend that favoritism to your relative, neither can you extend the same favoritism

**[02:55:52.780] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002948)  
to a person who stands in the same place as your relative.

**[02:55:58.480] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002949)  
Now, the Civil Service Commission does not come from nothing.

**[02:56:03.200] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002950)  
In the previous years, the Supreme Court has also declared as violative of the rule against

**[02:56:10.400] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002951)  
nepotism the designation of a relative.

**[02:56:13.560] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002952)  
Designation is not an appointment.

**[02:56:15.720] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002953)  
And designation is not also covered by the definition of the law on nepotism.

**[02:56:20.620] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002954)  
But the Supreme Court, using the same principle that what you cannot do directly, you cannot

**[02:56:25.500] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002955)  
do indirectly, also declared.

**[02:56:28.480] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002956)  
In the case of Laurel v. CSE, that if you cannot do that by way of appointment, you

**[02:56:33.280] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002957)  
cannot also do that by way of designation because the same evil exists.

**[02:56:37.440] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002958)  
Madam Chair, that's where the commission is coming from.

**[02:56:41.160] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002959)  
We do not want the same evil to exist.

**[02:56:45.300] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002960)  
We just use as our basis those previous pronouncements made by the Supreme Court.

**[02:56:51.760] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002961)  
What you cannot do directly, you cannot do indirectly.

**[02:56:54.000] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002962)  
Madam Chair.

**[02:56:57.150] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002963)  
Madam Chair, because we're referring this to the common-law partners.

**[02:57:03.790] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002969)  
Opo, kasi po yung common-law partners is in the same place as a married couple.

**[02:57:14.590] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002977)  
Um, so ang kulang lang po doon kasi ay marriage [unclear].

**[02:57:18.970] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002983)  
Pero I think the same relationship exists between and among the parties involved.

**[02:57:29.600] Rep. Ma. Isabel L. Sagarbarria** (asr-002995)  
Chair, this was only issued last year, right?

**[02:57:32.460] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002996)  
Hindi po last year

**[02:57:33.800] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002997)  
kasi iba po ang commission noon eh.

**[02:57:35.980] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002998)  
It was prior to the

**[02:57:37.400] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-002999)  
assumption of Chair

**[02:57:39.560] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003000)  
Yap as our Chair.

**[03:01:22.370] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003064)

**[03:01:25.830] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003065)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Mabilis na mabilis lang po to. As promised.

**[03:01:29.250] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003066)  
Firstly, Madam Chair Yap, maraming salamat po for supporting.

**[03:01:34.850] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003067)  
Yung kaninang query ni D.S. Ray Mendoza that you are supportive of public union doon po sa PSLMC. Napakahalaga po niya na hindi lang observer status eh, kundi a full-fledged member doon po sa PSLMC.

**[03:01:50.410] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003068)  
Ito pong natitirang tanong ko, Madam Chairperson. This pertains to a recent resolution or decision by the commission.

**[03:02:02.630] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003069)  
Noong 2019 po.

**[03:02:04.850] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003070)  
The Land Bank of the Philippines Employees Association filed a complaint of constructive demotion to the CSC.

**[03:02:12.710] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003071)  
Now, the commission dismissed that the removal of their allowances, benefits, and incentives amounting to 100,000 pesos were pursuant to the law and is not a demotion of rank.

**[03:02:29.210] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003072)  
Now, ang problem po dito, Madam Chairperson, po pwede nating sabihin na...

**[03:02:34.850] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003073)  
Hindi siya demotion, pero po para sa isang manggagawa na working class family po, yung 100,000 pesos po na annual loss, ay kasing sakit po ng demotion din.

**[03:02:50.330] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003074)  
So, to add fuel to the fire, Madam Chairperson, the case was filed in 2019.

**[03:02:56.870] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003075)  
Pero nagkaroon lang po ng resolution or decision ng CSC, 6 years.

**[03:03:04.850] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003076)  
After po na-file.

**[03:03:06.710] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003077)  
Ang tanong ko po dito, Madam Chair, ay una, bakit umabot ng limang taon ang delay sa resolution ng complaint ng Land Bank of the Philippines Employees Association?

**[03:03:17.390] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003078)  
And then second, moving forward, ano po yung mga pulisiya in place ng CSC para maiwasan na natin o mapigilan natin yung ganito katagal na backlog on disputes or cases, Madam Chairperson?

**[03:03:34.850] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003079)  
I think that was my first problem when I got into the Civil Service Commission.

**[03:03:44.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003080)  
Kasi hindi ko matanggap na yung isang administrative case magtatagal ng mahigit ng dalawang taon.

**[03:03:49.990] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003081)  
Kaya ang ginawa po namin, linaka po namin yung lahat ng inventory ng cases.

**[03:03:54.730] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003082)  
Tapos lahat ng pumapasok ay hindi tatagal ng mahigit 6 months kung pwede hanggang, depende sa complexity of the case.

**[03:04:02.810] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003083)  
And then we also adopted.

**[03:04:04.850] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003084)  
Ang mga regular na meron ng template na decisions, then we just study and then ilalapat na lang.

**[03:04:10.770] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003085)  
Ang ginawa rin po namin para matataas ang case resolution at saka case disposition is, nagbabacktrack po kami, may target kami na number going backward every time we have our commission meetings.

**[03:04:23.610] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003086)  
Tapos nag-segregate na kami ng commission meetings, hindi halo-halo, ang policy at saka yung case adjudication.

**[03:04:32.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003087)  
May dedicated na number.

**[03:04:34.850] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003088)  
Ang mga regular na session for all cases para mapabilis.

**[03:04:39.470] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003089)  
Tapos nag-establish kami ng mechanism para magkakaroon ng pre-commission meeting.

**[03:04:44.110] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003090)  
Lahat ng abogado ng tatlong executive offices.

**[03:04:48.110] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003091)  
Mirequire ko po yung fact sheet.

**[03:04:50.290] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003092)  
Parang tayo po ay nasa law school.

**[03:04:53.610] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003093)  
Para lahat po ng case file, meron ng fact sheet, i-recheck mo na lang.

**[03:04:56.870] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003094)  
Mas mapabilis yung pag-aaral dun sa decision na inire-recommend ng Office of Legal Affairs.

**[03:05:03.730] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003095)  
When we adopted.

**[03:05:04.850] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003096)  
Tapos mechanism na pinaigsian yung review, pero mas matindi ang review sa Office of Legal Affairs.

**[03:05:10.750] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003097)  
And then we go to the executive committee meetings through a series of pre-commission meetings

**[03:05:15.310] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003098)  
para mas mabilis yung action ng commission.

**[03:05:18.150] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003099)  
So those are the measures that we have done.

**[03:05:20.710] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003100)  
If you look at our record from end 2024 up to 2026,

**[03:05:25.150] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003101)  
yung dami po na na-dispose namin na cases compared to the previous years ay medyo malayo na po.

**[03:05:31.390] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003102)  
Ang target namin is within the end of the year.

**[03:05:34.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003103)  
Yung backlog namin sa mga kapapasok lang na mga kaso ay ma-reduce to at least 10% or 5%.

**[03:05:41.270] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003104)  
Maiiwan na lang pala 10% or 5%.

**[03:05:43.970] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003105)  
Yung sa aging cases, kailangan naming hukayin.

**[03:05:47.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003106)  
Pero I would think, depending on the numbers, ay pwede nating hukayin.

**[03:05:53.410] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003107)  
Medyo dumami-dami na yun.

**[03:05:55.930] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003108)  
Ang naging problema rin po before, hindi problema actually, but that is how processes work.

**[03:06:00.590] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003109)  
Parang kinopia kasi yung adjudication system.

**[03:06:04.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003110)  
The judiciary na hindi inilalabas yung decision hanggang hindi prinopromulgate.

**[03:06:10.170] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003111)  
Pero ang sinasabi ko ngayon is ipagdugtungin natin yung pinag-usapan namin sa meetings namin is

**[03:06:18.010] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003112)  
basta in-approve ng commission at subject na lang sa editing, pwede na namin siyang ilabas.

**[03:06:24.190] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003113)  
Tapos pwede namin ilagay doon yung upon effectivity.

**[03:06:27.950] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003114)  
It can be a number of choices.

**[03:06:30.390] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003115)  
Depende sa klase ng kaso.

**[03:06:32.290] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003116)  
Effectivity upon approval of the commission.

**[03:06:34.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003117)  
Or after 10 to 15 days of publication in the official gazette.

**[03:06:40.170] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003118)  
So those are the remedial measures that we have done.

**[03:06:42.930] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003119)  
Hopefully, if the project of ASCOM Ariel, which is the CLAS, which is the Civil Service Legal and Administrative Services Electronic System,

**[03:06:52.470] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003120)  
baka mas mapabilis na po ang disposition ng cases.

**[03:06:55.150] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003121)  
Kasi masakit po talaga sa akin yung matagal na disposition of cases.

**[03:06:58.830] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003122)  
Naging maliit din po ako na empleyado ng gobyerno.

**[03:07:01.650] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003123)  
I rose from the ranks, so I know how it feels.

**[03:07:04.070] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003124)  
If you have these pending cases.

**[03:07:06.010] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003125)  
Sa disciplinary board ka nga lang po kung dalhin, masakit na eh.

**[03:07:10.450] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003126)  
So we'll try very hard, sir, to make our case disposition faster.

**[03:07:20.130] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003127)  
Thank you, Madam Chairperson.

**[03:07:22.330] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003128)  
Magandang bagay po na merong admission po na meron talagang problema pagdating po sa case disposition.

**[03:07:29.450] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003129)  
Kasi ito pong mga manggagawa ng Land Bank of the Philippines.

**[03:07:33.090] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003130)  
Ilang taon na sila.

**[03:07:34.070] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003131)  
Nag-antay.

**[03:07:34.670] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003132)  
And I commend the CSC na meron po kayong mga measures, corrective measures in place at yung mga pangmatagalan nating solusyon.

**[03:07:42.970] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003133)  
Doon po sa binabanggit nyo yung CLAS, magkano po ang nirecommend na budget allocation for 2027?

**[03:07:51.290] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003134)  
And how will that impact po yung pagsasapabilis ng mga case disposition natin?

**[03:08:00.860] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003135)  
Ask ko

**[03:08:01.260] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003136)  
Magkano daw yung budget?

**[03:08:03.240] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003137)  
Naka-charge sa regular ISSP budget.

**[03:08:05.480] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003138)  
Paki check nyo lang.

**[03:08:07.980] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003139)  
Ibigay nyo kayo, sir.

**[03:08:10.560] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003140)  
Can we provide you with a figure?

**[03:08:12.740] CSC Chairperson Marilyn B. Yap** (asr-003141)  
Hanapin lang po sandali

**[03:08:16.620] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003142)  
Opo.

**[03:08:17.020] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003143)  
And as to how it can hasten the disposition of the case, well, unang-unang po, one of the features of the CLAS is to allow the online filing of pleadings from our public.

**[03:08:30.660] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003144)  
And then from the online filing of pleadings, there will be online transmission of all the documents that we are receiving.

**[03:08:36.660] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003145)  
From the receiving section to the record section of the OLA, to the divisions and the action officers handling the case, everything will be digitalized.

**[03:08:48.300] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003146)  
E-scan po yun so that they will be working on documents na hindi na po physical.

**[03:08:52.540] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003147)  
They will just be looking at the scan copy and then they will be working on those documents.

**[03:08:59.040] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003148)  
Pangalawa po, if there are records that we need, for example, from our regional offices, through the CLAS po.

**[03:09:06.420] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003149)  
Kasi interconnected na po lahat ng offices namin.

**[03:09:09.460] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003150)  
Hindi na po kami maghihintay ng matagal na panahon just to receive the records.

**[03:09:13.120] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003151)  
They can transmit the records to us online so that in no time, we can receive the records that we need so that we can finally dispose of the case.

**[03:09:23.140] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003152)  
Naka-involve na rin po sa CLAS ang lahat ng opisina ng commission sa central office.

**[03:09:27.860] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003153)  
So pati po yung transmission from OLA to our secretariat and then eventually to the executive offices.

**[03:09:34.220] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003154)  
Mas mabilis na po lahat yun.

**[03:09:36.420] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003155)  
Everything can be acted upon with dispatch.

**[03:09:39.580] CSC Assistant Commissioner Ariel Ronquillo** (asr-003156)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:09:40.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003157)  
Thank you. Maraming salamat po.

**[03:09:42.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003158)  
As part po ng mga hinihingi namin na papers, makikihingi na lang din po ang committee ng current inventory of pending administrative complaints po para makita rin natin gano'ng karamihan.

**[03:09:55.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003159)  
Madam Chairperson, yun lang naman po yung mga katanungan, ang kinatawang ito.

**[03:09:59.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003160)  
Sa bahagi po ng minoriya, Madam Chair, wala na pong magtatanong. Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[03:10:06.420] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003161)  
Salamat, kaibigan. Follow-up questions lang.

**[03:10:09.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-003162)
